## Session 631 - 2025-12-16

### Session Summary - い Pattern Complete ✓
**Status**: Implemented 1 NT pattern successfully
**Patterns completed**: 1 (い - NT, priority 6)
**Total tests**: 3999 passing (3 new tests, no regressions)
**Progress**: Fourth NT pattern completed! 🎉

**Session highlights**:
- Implemented い sentence-ending particle pattern
- Pattern matches だい and わい variants (かい already covered by N4 pattern)
- Detects particles that add familiarity and friendliness to speech
- All structure variants tested with realistic conversational sentences
- All tests pass with proper range assertions

### ✓ Completed: い (sentence-ending particle for friendliness/familiarity)

**Grammar Point**: い
**JLPT Level**: NT
**Priority**: 6
**Register**: Casual

**Pattern structures**:
- Phrase + わい (e.g., するわい - friendly/familiar emphasis)
- Phrase + だい (e.g., 大変だい - friendly/familiar emphasis)
- Note: かい is handled by a separate N4 pattern

**Meaning**:
- Sentence-ending particle that adds familiarity and friendliness
- Usually only used by men when speaking to someone of lower social position (age difference)
- Most commonly paired with か (→かい) or だ (→だい)
- Less commonly paired with わ (→わい), や, ぞ, or imperative verbs
- Softens the tone and makes speech more approachable

**Tokenization pattern**:
**わい variant:**
- [Verb/Adjective/Auxiliary]
- わい (助詞/終助詞)

**だい variant:**
- [Noun/な-Adjective]
- だい (名詞/一般)

**Implementation**:
Created 3 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. WaiMatcher - Matches わい (助詞/終助詞)
2. DaiMatcher - Matches だい (名詞/一般)
3. IMatcher - Combined matcher for わい or だい

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding word - verb, adjective, auxiliary verb, noun)
- IMatcher (わい or だい)

**Tests Written: 3 tests (all passing)**
1. test_i_wai - するわい (11, 15) - verb + わい
2. test_i_dai - 大変だい (3, 7) - な-adjective + だい
3. test_i_wai_variant - 行くわい (3, 7) - verb + わい

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full construction (word + い-particle):
- するわい (11, 15) - 4 characters
- 大変だい (3, 7) - 4 characters
- 行くわい (3, 7) - 4 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern focuses on わい and だい variants
- かい is already handled by a separate N4 pattern (higher specificity)
- Tokenization varies: わい is 助詞/終助詞, だい is 名詞/一般
- IMatcher combines both variants for comprehensive detection
- Priority set to 6 (same as other sentence-ending particles)
- Simple structure: Any + particle

**Files Modified:**
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/nt.rs - Implemented i() with 3 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs - Updated priority to 6
- grammar-lib/src/tests/nt_patterns.rs - Added 3 tests in i_tests module
- feature_list.json - Updated い to complete status

**Quality Checks**:

✓ All detectable structure variants tested (わい, だい)
✓ Realistic conversational sentences used from grammar_points_data.json
✓ Correct character ranges verified
✓ No print_debug() in final committed tests
✓ All existing tests still pass (3996 + 3 = 3999 total)
✓ Clean git history

- つ (Slang) - Slang particle

### ✓ Completed: ものとして (supposing that / on the assumption that)

**Grammar Point**: ものとして
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + ものとして (e.g., できるものとして - on the assumption that [someone] can)
2. い-Adjective + ものとして (e.g., 難しいものとして - on the assumption that it's difficult)
3. な-Adjective + な + ものとして (e.g., 不可能なものとして - assuming it's impossible)
4. Noun + である + ものとして (e.g., 事実であるものとして - assuming it's a fact)

**Meaning**:
- "Supposing that (A)" / "On the assumption that (A)" / "Treating (A) as true"
- Combination of もの (thing/matter) + として (as/in capacity of)
- The もの makes the preceding phrase into a strong determination
- Used when determining (A) is true for the sake of discussion
- More formal than just として - adds a sense of stiffness or formality

**Tokenization pattern**:
- [Verb/Adjective/Noun + modifiers]
- もの (名詞/非自立/一般)
- として (助詞/格助詞/連語)

**Implementation**:
Created 2 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. MonoMatcher - Matches もの (名詞/非自立/一般)
2. ToshiteMatcher - Matches として (助詞/格助詞/連語)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (matches the token immediately before もの)
- MonoMatcher (もの)
- ToshiteMatcher (として)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_monotoshite_verb_not_attend - ないものとして (14, 21) - verb + negative
2. test_monotoshite_verb_can_participate - できるものとして (6, 14) - potential verb
3. test_monotoshite_i_adjective_difficult - 難しいものとして (7, 15) - い-adjective
4. test_monotoshite_na_adjective_impossible - なものとして (10, 16) - な-adjective + な
5. test_monotoshite_noun_dearu - あるものとして (6, 13) - noun + である

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures token before もの + もの + として:
- ないものとして (14, 21) - 7 characters
- できるものとして (6, 14) - 8 characters
- 難しいものとして (7, 15) - 8 characters
- なものとして (10, 16) - 6 characters
- あるものとして (6, 13) - 7 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Uses TokenMatcher::Any to match the immediate predecessor of もの
- For single-token cases (verbs, い-adjectives), captures the full word
- For multi-token phrases (verb compounds, な-adjective + な, noun + である), captures the last token
- Priority set to 5 for proper detection hierarchy
- Simple, elegant implementation - only 2 custom matchers needed
- Example sentences directly from grammar_points_data.json

**Session highlights**:

- Created 6 custom matchers to handle all tokenization patterns
- All structure variants tested with realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json
- Tokenization: を + ぜん + てい + に/にして/として

### ✓ Completed: を前提に (on the premise of / on the assumption that)

**Grammar Point**: を前提に
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + を前提に (e.g., 結婚をぜんていに - on the premise of marriage)
2. Verb + こと + を前提に (e.g., 使われることをぜんていに - on the premise of being used)
3. Noun/こと + をぜんていにして (e.g., ことをぜんていにして - toward the premise that)
4. Noun/こと + をぜんていとして (e.g., ことをぜんていとして - with the premise that)

**Meaning**:
- "On the premise of (A), (B)" / "On the assumption that (A), (B)"
- Combination of を (particle) + 前提 (premise/hypothesis) + に/にして/として
- Marks something as the base thought/goal toward which another action is taken
- Used with nouns or nominalized verbs (using こと)

**Tokenization pattern**:
- [Noun or こと]
- を (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- ぜん (名詞/一般)
- てい (名詞/一般)
- に (助詞/格助詞) OR として (助詞/格助詞/連語)
- Optional: し (動詞/自立, base=する, 連用形) + て (助詞/接続助詞) for にして variant

**Implementation**:
Created 6 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. WoMatcher - Matches を (助詞/格助詞/一般)
2. ZenMatcher - Matches ぜん (名詞/一般, base=ぜん)
3. TeiMatcher - Matches てい (名詞/一般, base=てい)
4. NiOrToshiteMatcher - Matches に OR として (both 助詞/格助詞)
5. ShiMatcher - Optional, matches し (動詞/自立, base=する, 連用形) for にして variant
6. TeMatcher - Optional, matches て (助詞/接続助詞) for にして variant

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding noun or こと)
- WoMatcher (を)
- ZenMatcher (ぜん)
- TeiMatcher (てい)
- NiOrToshiteMatcher (に or として)
- Optional(ShiMatcher) (し)
- Optional(TeMatcher) (て)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_wozenteini_noun_marriage - 結婚をぜんていに (5, 13) - noun form
2. test_wozenteini_verb_koto_workplace - ことをぜんていに (15, 23) - verb + こと
3. test_wozenteini_verb_koto_racing - ことをぜんていに (13, 21) - verb + こと
4. test_wozenteini_nishite_variant - ことをぜんていにして (10, 20) - にして variant
5. test_wozenteini_toshite_variant - ことをぜんていとして (13, 23) - として variant

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full construction (noun/こと + を + ぜん + てい + に/にして/として):
- 結婚をぜんていに (5, 13) - 8 characters
- ことをぜんていに (15, 23) - 8 characters
- ことをぜんていにして (10, 20) - 10 characters
- ことをぜんていとして (13, 23) - 10 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (full grammatical construction)
- Works with any noun or nominalized verb (こと)
- NiOrToshiteMatcher handles both に and として endings
- Optional matchers allow にして variant to be detected
- Priority set to 5 for proper detection hierarchy
- Example sentences directly from grammar_points_data.json

- Used to present a topic (A) and explain speaker's instinctive thoughts (B)
- All structure variants tested with realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json

### ✓ Completed: と来たら (when it comes to / concerning)

**Grammar Point**: と来たら
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structure**:
- Phrase + ときたら (only 1 structure variant)

**Meaning**:
- "When it comes to (A), (B)" / "Concerning (A), (B)"
- Literal translation: と (case-marking particle) + 来たら (conditional of 来る "to come")
- Used to present a topic (A) and then explain speaker's instinctive thoughts about (B)
- (A) is something that strongly makes the speaker think of (B)
- The relationship between (A) and (B) may be obvious or go without saying
- Often appears with やっぱり or やはり "sure enough" in the (B) phrase

**Key difference from ときたら (kana)**:
- ときたら (kana): Casual, expressing frustration "that darn (A)"
- と来たら (kanji): Standard, neutral, "when it comes to (A)"

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Phrase (noun, adjective + noun, etc.)
- と (助詞/格助詞/一般 or 引用)
- 来 (動詞/自立, base=来る, カ変・来ル, 連用形)
- たら (助動詞, base=た, 特殊・タ, 仮定形)

**Implementation**:
Created 3 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. ToMatcher - Matches と (助詞/格助詞)
2. KiMatcher - Matches 来 (動詞/自立, base=来る, 連用形)
3. TaraMatcher - Matches たら (助動詞, base=た, 仮定形)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding phrase)
- ToMatcher (と)
- KiMatcher (来)
- TaraMatcher (たら)

**Tests Written: 3 tests (all passing)**
1. test_tokitara2_summer_beer - 夏と来たら (0, 5) - noun + ときたら
2. test_tokitara2_cold_day_hotpot - 日と来たら (2, 7) - adjective + noun + ときたら
3. test_tokitara2_harassment_problem - パワハラと来たら (0, 8) - noun + ときたら

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full construction (phrase + と来たら):
- 夏と来たら (0, 5) - 5 characters
- 日と来たら (2, 7) - 5 characters
- パワハラと来たら (0, 8) - 8 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding phrase (full grammatical construction)
- Works with any type of phrase before と来たら
- Different tokenization from ときたら (kana) which uses とき verb
- More neutral and standard register than ときたら
- Priority set to 5 for proper detection hierarchy
- Example sentences directly from grammar_points_data.json

## Session 625 - 2025-12-16

### Session Summary - 始末だ Complete ✓
**Status**: Implemented 1 N1 pattern successfully
**Patterns completed**: 1 (始末だ - N1, priority 5)
**Total tests**: 3968 passing (4 new tests, no regressions)
**Progress**: One hundred and thirty-seventh N1 pattern completed! 🎉

**Session highlights**:
- Implemented 始末だ (wind up as / end up as / culminate in - negative outcome)
- Pattern used to indicate negative sequences of events leading to a bad result
- Handles both Verb[る] + 始末だ and この + 始末だ structures
- All structure variants tested with realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json
- Tokenization splits しまつ into し (classical auxiliary き) + まつ (noun) + だ (copula)

### ✓ Completed: 始末だ (wind up as / end up as / culminate in)

**Grammar Point**: 始末だ
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[る] + 始末だ (e.g., 帰るしまつだ - ended up leaving)
2. この + 始末だ (e.g., このしまつだ - this is the result)

**Meaning**:
- "To wind up as (A)" / "To end up as (A)" / "To come about that (A)" / "To culminate in (A)"
- 始末（しまつ）is a noun meaning "settlement" or "end result"
- Used to indicate a negative sequence of events causing an equally negative result
- Emphasizes the culmination of events from beginning to end
- Often appears with words like とうとう (to come to a head), 挙げ句 (after a long process), or 結局 (in the end)
- Always carries a negative connotation about the outcome

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Verb (基本形) OR この (連体詞)
- し (助動詞, base=き, 文語・キ, 体言接続) - classical auxiliary
- まつ (名詞/一般, base=まつ) - noun "matsu"
- だ (助動詞, base=だ, 特殊・ダ, 基本形) - copula

**Implementation**:
Created 3 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. ShiMatcher - Matches し (助動詞, base=き, 文語・キ, 体言接続)
2. MatsuMatcher - Matches まつ (名詞/一般, base=まつ)
3. DaMatcher - Matches だ (助動詞, base=だ, 特殊・ダ)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (verb or この)
- ShiMatcher (し)
- MatsuMatcher (まつ)
- DaMatcher (だ)

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_shimatsuda_kono_doublecheck - このしまつだ (23, 29) - この variant
2. test_shimatsuda_verb_festival - 行くしまつだ (37, 43) - verb + しまつだ
3. test_shimatsuda_verb_store - 帰るしまつだ (27, 33) - verb + しまつだ

### ✓ Completed: べくもない (impossible to / no way to)

**Grammar Point**: べくもない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + べくもない (standard negative)
2. Verb + べくもなかった (past tense)
3. する-Verb + す + べくもない (exception for する verbs)
4. Verb + べくもありません (polite form)

**Meaning**:
- "It is impossible to (A)" / "There is no way to (A)" / "Cannot possibly (A)"
- Classical auxiliary verb べし (shall) in conjunctive form べく
- Combined with も (adverbial particle) + ない (adjective)
- Used with verbs of thinking/emotion to express impossibility
- The speaker expresses how an action is impossible or inconceivable for them

**Tokenization pattern**:
**Standard form (Verb + べくもない):**
- Verb (基本形 or 文語基本形)
- べく (助動詞, base=べし, 文語・ベシ, 連用形)
- も (助詞/係助詞)
- ない (形容詞/自立, 形容詞・アウオ段, 基本形)

**Polite form (Verb + べくもありません):**
- Verb (基本形)
- べく (助動詞, base=べし, 文語・ベシ, 連用形)
- も (助詞/係助詞)
- あり (動詞/自立, base=ある, 五段・ラ行, 連用形)
- ませ (助動詞, base=ます, 特殊・マス, 未然形)
- ん (助動詞, base=ん, 不変化型, 基本形)

**する exception:**
- す (動詞/自立, base=する, サ変・スル, 文語基本形)

**Implementation**:
Created 6 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. BekuMatcher - Matches べく (助動詞, base=べし, 連用形)
2. MoMatcher - Matches も (助詞/係助詞)
3. NaiOrAriMatcher - Matches ない/なかっ (形容詞) OR あり (動詞, base=ある)
4. MaseMatcher - Optional, matches ませ (助動詞, base=ます) for polite form
5. NMatcher - Optional, matches ん (助動詞) for polite negative
6. TaMatcher - Optional, matches た (助動詞) for past tense

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (verb including する-verbs with す stem)
- BekuMatcher (べく)
- MoMatcher (も)
- NaiOrAriMatcher (ない/なかっ/あり)
- Optional(MaseMatcher) (ませ)
- Optional(NMatcher) (ん)
- Optional(TaMatcher) (た)

**Tests Written: 6 tests (all passing)**

2. test_bekumonai_doubt - 疑うべくもない (23, 30) - no doubt

4. test_bekumonai_suru_verb_exception - 否定するべくもない (30, 39) - する verb
5. test_bekumonai_suru_verb_su_stem - 解決すべくもない (11, 19) - す stem
6. test_bekumonai_polite_form - 想像するべくもありません (13, 25) - polite form

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures verb + べくもない/ありません:
- 比べるべくもない (24, 32) - 8 characters
- 疑うべくもない (23, 30) - 7 characters
- 知るべくもなかった (21, 30) - 9 characters (past)
- 否定するべくもない (30, 39) - 9 characters (する verb)
- 解決すべくもない (11, 19) - 8 characters (す stem)
- 想像するべくもありません (13, 25) - 12 characters (polite)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the full grammatical construction (verb + べくもない/ありません)
- NaiOrAriMatcher handles both casual (ない) and polite (あり) forms
- Works with する-verbs using both する and す stems
- Handles past tense (なかった) with optional TaMatcher

### ✓ Completed: ものなら② (if you were to / if one happens to)

**Grammar Point**: ものなら②
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structure**:
- Verb[volitional] + ものなら/もんなら + Phrase

**Meaning**:
- "If you were to (A), (B)" / "If (A) happens, (B)"
- (A) is in volitional form, (B) is a negative consequence
- Volitional form implies reckless abandon / proceeding without considering outcome
- Literal: "If the thing of (A) were"
- Combination of もの/もん (noun: thing) + なら (hypothetical form of だ)

**Key difference from ものなら①**:
- ものなら①: Uses potential/できる form (if one could, but it's not thought possible)
- ものなら②: Uses volitional form (if you were to recklessly do, negative consequence)

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Verb (未然ウ接続) - verb stem ready for volitional う
- う (助動詞, 不変化型) - volitional auxiliary
- もの/もん (名詞/非自立/一般) - "thing"
- なら (助動詞, base=だ, 仮定形) - hypothetical "if"

**Implementation**:
Created 3 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. VolitionalUMatcher - Matches う (助動詞, 不変化型)
2. MonoOrMonMatcher - Matches もの or もん (名詞/非自立/一般)
3. NaraMatcher - Matches なら (助動詞, base=だ, 仮定形)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::verb_with_form("未然ウ接続") - verb in volitional form
- VolitionalUMatcher (う)
- MonoOrMonMatcher (もの/もん)
- NaraMatcher (なら)

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**

2. test_mononara_u2461_forget_report - 忘れようものなら (9, 17) - if I happen to forget
3. test_mononara_u2461_go_under_fire - しようものなら (10, 19) - if we happen to
4. test_mononara_u2461_monnara_variant - 言おうもんなら (5, 12) - casual もんなら variant

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures verb + volitional + ものなら/もんなら:
- 忘れようものなら (7, 15) - 8 characters
- 言おうもんなら (5, 12) - 7 characters (もんなら is 1 character shorter)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the full grammatical construction (verb + う + もの/もん + なら)
- MonoOrMonMatcher handles both standard もの and casual もん
- VolitionalUMatcher specifically matches う auxiliary (not just surface "う")
- Used when negative consequences follow from reckless action
- Priority set to 5 for proper detection hierarchy

### ✓ Completed: にひきかえ (in stark contrast to / in comparison to)

**Grammar Point**: にひきかえ
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + にひきかえ (e.g., 去年の夏にひきかえ - in contrast to last year's summer)
2. な-Adjective + な + の + にひきかえ (e.g., 裕福なのにひきかえ - in contrast to being wealthy)
3. い-Adjective + の + にひきかえ (e.g., いいのにひきかえ - in contrast to being good)
4. Verb + の + にひきかえ (e.g., 就職しているのにひきかえ - in contrast to getting employed)
5. それ + にひきかえ (sentence-initial, e.g., それにひきかえ - in contrast to that)

**Meaning**:
- "In stark contrast to (A), (B)" / "In comparison to (A), (B)"
- Literal translation: "pulling back and switching away from (A), (B)"
- The 'switching away from' portion emphasizes the large size of the difference
- Combination of に (case-marking particle) + 引く (to pull, conjunctive form) + 換える (to switch/interchange, conjunctive form)
- Both (A) and (B) are opposite ends of some spectrum, where one is good and the other is bad (subjective)
- Only used with plainly observable or measurable facts (not assumptions or guesses)

**Tokenization pattern**:
- [Any preceding word: Noun, の, それ, etc.]
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- ひきかえ (動詞/自立, base=ひきかえる, 一段, 連用形)

**Implementation**:
Created 2 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NiMatcher - Matches に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
2. HikikaeruMatcher - Matches ひきかえ (動詞/自立, base=ひきかえる, 連用形)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding word)
- NiMatcher (に)
- HikikaeruMatcher (ひきかえ)

4. test_nihikikae_verb - のにひきかえ (20, 26) - verb + の
5. test_nihikikae_sorenihikikae - それにひきかえ (26, 33) - それ variant

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures preceding word + にひきかえ:
- 夏にひきかえ (3, 9) - 6 characters (noun)
- のにひきかえ (9, 15) - 6 characters (の variant for adj/verb)
- それにひきかえ (26, 33) - 7 characters (それ variant)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (full grammatical construction)
- Works with any word type before にひきかえ (verbs, adjectives, nouns)
- の is used to nominalize verbs and adjectives before adding にひきかえ
- それにひきかえ is used sentence-initially to refer to a previous statement
- Used to emphasize strong contrasts between observable facts
- The contrasted items are subjectively evaluated as opposite (good vs bad)
- Priority set to 5 for proper detection hierarchy

**Session highlights**:
- Implemented ようによっては (depending on the way that / depending on how)
- Complex pattern with THREE different tokenization variants
- Handles volitional form (見よう), verb stem + よう suffix (混みよう), and noun + よう (使いよう)
- All structure variants tested with realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json
- Created flexible matcher using YouOrUMatcher to handle both よう (noun suffix) and う (volitional auxiliary)

### ✓ Completed: ようによっては (depending on the way that)

**Grammar Point**: ようによっては
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb(volitional) + によって + は - e.g., 見ようによっては (depending on how you look at it)
2. Verb(連用形) + よう + によって + は - e.g., 混みようによっては (depending on how crowded it is)
3. Noun + よう + によって + は - e.g., 使いようによっては (depending on how you use it)

**Meaning**:
- "Depending on the way that (A), (B)" / "Depending on how (A)"
- Combination of 様(よう) 'manner/way' + に (particle) + よって (て-form of よる 'to depend on') + は (adverbial particle)
- Often highlights results that vary greatly from person to person or from one method to another
- More formal version of 方(かた)によっては

**Tokenization patterns**:
**Pattern 1** (volitional form like 見よう):
- Verb (未然ウ接続) + う (助動詞, 不変化型) + によって (助詞/格助詞/連語) + は (助詞/係助詞)

**Pattern 2** (verb stem + suffix like 混みよう):
- Verb (連用形) + よう (名詞/接尾/一般) + によって (助詞/格助詞/連語) + は (助詞/係助詞)

**Pattern 3** (noun + suffix like 使いよう):
- Noun + よう (名詞/接尾/一般) + によって (助詞/格助詞/連語) + は (助詞/係助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created 3 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. YouOrUMatcher - Matches EITHER よう (名詞/接尾/一般) OR う (助動詞) - mutually exclusive alternatives
2. NiyotteMatcher - Matches によって (助詞/格助詞/連語)
3. WaParticleMatcher - Matches は (助詞/係助詞)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (verb or noun)
- YouOrUMatcher (よう or う)
- NiyotteMatcher (によって)
- WaParticleMatcher (は)

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_youniyotteha_verb_watch - 見ようによっては (5, 13) - volitional form
2. test_youniyotteha_verb_congestion - 混みようによっては (13, 22) - verb stem + よう
3. test_youniyotteha_verb_use - 使いようによっては (14, 23) - noun + よう
4. test_youniyotteha_verb_look - 見ようによっては (4, 12) - volitional form

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full construction (verb/noun + よう/う + によって + は):
- 見ようによっては (5, 13) - 8 characters (volitional)
- 混みようによっては (13, 22) - 9 characters (verb stem + よう)
- 使いようによっては (14, 23) - 9 characters (noun + よう)

**Key Notes**:
- Most complex tokenization pattern implemented - handles 3 distinct structural variants
- YouOrUMatcher is key innovation: matches EITHER よう suffix OR volitional う
- Pattern includes the preceding word (full grammatical construction)
- Works with verbs in volitional form AND with よう as a noun suffix
- Priority set to 5 for proper detection hierarchy
- Example sentences directly from grammar_points_data.json

### ✓ Completed: にかこつけて (under the pretense of / using as an excuse)

**Grammar Point**: にかこつけて
**JLPT Level**: N1

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + にかこつけて (e.g., 風邪にかこつけて - using a cold as an excuse)
2. Verb + の + にかこつけて (e.g., 行くというのにかこつけて - under the pretense of going)

**Meaning**:
- "Under the pretense of (A)" / "Using (A) as an excuse" / "Labeling it as (A)"
- Based on case-marking particle に + る-Verb かこつける "to consign as"
- Used primarily for criticism of surface-level excuses used to fulfill an ulterior motive
- The original meaning of "to consign" works as "labeling it as (A)"

**Tokenization pattern**:
- [Noun or の] (preceding word)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- かこつけ (動詞/自立, base=かこつける, 一段, 連用形)
- て (助詞/接続助詞)

1. NiMatcher - Matches に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
2. KakotsukeMatcher - Matches かこつけ (base=かこつける, 動詞/自立, 連用形)
3. TeMatcher - Matches て (助詞/接続助詞)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding noun or の)
- NiMatcher (に)
- KakotsukeMatcher (かこつけ)
- TeMatcher (て)

- Handles both present (ない) and past (なかった) tense variations
- Pattern works with Nouns and nominalized verbs (Verb + の)

### ✓ Completed: を禁じ得ない (cannot help feeling / cannot hold back from)

**Grammar Point**: を禁じ得ない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + を禁じ得ない (standard negative)
2. Verb + の + を禁じ得ない (nominalized verb)
3. Noun + を禁じ得ません (polite negative)
4. Verb + の + を禁じ得ません (nominalized verb, polite)

**Meaning**:
- "Cannot help feeling (A)" / "Cannot hold back from (A)"
- Formal expression showing inability to suppress emotions or reactions
- Combination of を (object marker) + 禁じる (to prohibit/forbid) + 得ない (cannot obtain)
- Literal translation: "cannot prohibit (A)"
- Primarily used with words expressing emotions: 不安 (anxiety), 怒り (anger), 涙 (tears), 感動 (being moved)
- Almost exclusively used for first-person opinions (expressing one's own feelings)

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Noun/の (preceding word)
- を (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- きんじ (動詞/自立, base=きんじる, 一段, 連用形)
- え (動詞/非自立, base=える, 一段, 未然形 or 連用形)
- ない/なかっ (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ) OR ませ (助動詞, 特殊・マス)
- Optional: ん (助動詞, 不変化型) for polite negative

**Implementation**:
Created 5 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. WoMatcher - Matches を (助詞/格助詞/一般)
2. KinjiMatcher - Matches きんじ (base=きんじる, 動詞/自立, 連用形)
3. EMatcher - Matches え (base=える, 動詞/非自立, 未然形 or 連用形)
4. NaiOrMaseMatcher - Matches ない/なかっ (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ) OR ませ (助動詞, 特殊・マス)
5. NMatcher - Optional, matches ん (助動詞, 不変化型) for polite negative

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding noun or の)
- WoMatcher (を)
- KinjiMatcher (きんじ)
- EMatcher (え)
- NaiOrMaseMatcher (ない/なかっ/ませ)
- Optional(NMatcher) (ん)

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_wokinjienai_noun_standard - 不安をきんじえない (18, 27) - noun + casual
2. test_wokinjienai_verb_nominalized_standard - のをきんじえなかった (28, 38) - verb + の + past
3. test_wokinjienai_noun_polite - 怒りをきんじえません (28, 38) - noun + polite
4. test_wokinjienai_verb_nominalized_polite - のをきんじえませんでした (12, 24) - verb + の + polite past

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full construction (Noun/の + を + 禁じ得ない):
- 不安をきんじえない (18, 27) - 9 characters
- のをきんじえなかった (28, 38) - 10 characters (with past tense)
- 怒りをきんじえません (28, 38) - 10 characters (polite)
- のをきんじえませんでした (12, 24) - 12 characters (polite past)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding noun or の (full grammatical construction)
- EMatcher handles both 未然形 (for ない) and 連用形 (for ます)
- NaiOrMaseMatcher handles both casual (ない/なかっ) and polite (ませ) forms
- Added support for past tense (なかっ) which appears in subtitle-quality sentences
- Used with emotion nouns: 不安, 怒り, 涙, 感動, etc.
- Only used for first-person expressions (sounds unnatural for third-person)

### ✓ Completed: ならいざ知らず (I don't know about A, but B)

**Grammar Point**: ならいざ知らず
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + ならいざ知らず (most common)
2. Verb + ならいざ知らず
3. い-Adjective + の + ならいざ知らず (nominalized)
4. な-Adjective + ならいざ知らず
5. Noun + はいざ知らず (は variant)

**Meaning**:
- "I don't know about (A), but (B)" / "Maybe (A), but (B)"
- Formal expression emphasizing that regardless of (A), (B) fits a specific description
- Combination of なら (hypothetical form of だ) + いざ (adverb: "now"/"well then") + 知らず (classical negative of 知る "to know")
- Literal translation: "if it's (A), well then I don't know"
- Emphasizes that (A) may or may not be the case, but (B) is definite
- Often (A) is an extreme example showing (B) is the opposite, making it surprising or irritating

**Tokenization patterns**:
**Core pattern (all variants):**
- なら (助動詞, base=だ, 仮定形) OR は (助詞/係助詞)
- いざ (副詞/一般)
- しら (動詞/自立, base=しる, 未然形)
- ず (助動詞, base=ぬ, 連用ニ接続)

**Preceding word:** Any token (Noun, Verb, Adjective, or の)

**Implementation**:
Created 4 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NaraOrWaMatcher - Matches なら (助動詞, base=だ, 仮定形) OR は (助詞/係助詞)
2. IzaMatcher - Matches いざ (副詞/一般)
3. ShiraMatcher - Matches しら (動詞/自立, base=しる, 未然形)
4. ZuMatcher - Matches ず (助動詞, base=ぬ, 連用ニ接続)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding word)
- NaraOrWaMatcher (なら or は)
- IzaMatcher (いざ)
- ShiraMatcher (しら)
- ZuMatcher (ず)

- NaraOrWaMatcher handles both なら (most common) and は variants
- Works with any word type before the pattern (verbs, adjectives, nouns, の)
- の is used to nominalize verbs and adjectives before adding ならいざ知らず
- Often used with extreme examples to show contrast (e.g., "gods maybe, but not regular people")

### ✓ Completed: には及ばない② (not as good as / no match for)

**Grammar Point**: には及ばない②
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + には及ばない (standard negative)
2. Noun + には及びません (polite negative)
3. Noun + の足元にも及ばない (set expression: "not even reach the feet of")

**Meaning**:
- "Not as good as (A)" / "No match for (A)" / "Cannot be compared to (A)"
- Shows that something doesn't reach the level/extent of something else
- Combination of に (case-marking particle) + は (adverbial particle) + 及ぶ (to reach) + ない (negative)
- Literal translation: "does not extend to (A)"
- Used when (A) is being given extremely high praise
- Different from には及ばない① which means "there is no need to"

**Tokenization patterns**:
**Standard form (にはおよばない):**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- およば (動詞/自立, base=およぶ, 未然形)
- ない (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ)

**Polite form (にはおよびません):**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- および (動詞/自立, base=およぶ, 連用形)
- ませ (助動詞, 特殊・マス, 未然形)
- ん (助動詞, 不変化型)

**Set expression (にもおよばない):**
- Same structure but も instead of は

**Implementation**:
Created 6 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞/一般
2. WaOrMoMatcher - Matches は or も as 助詞/係助詞
3. OyobuMatcher - Matches およば (未然形) or および (連用形), base=およぶ
4. NaiMaseMatcher - Matches ない (助動詞) or ませ (助動詞)
5. NMatcher - Optional, matches ん (助動詞) for polite negative

Pattern matcher sequence:
- super::noun_matcher() (preceding noun)
- NiMatcher (に)
- WaOrMoMatcher (は or も)
- OyobuMatcher (およば/および)
- NaiMaseMatcher (ない/ませ)
- Optional(NMatcher) (ん)

- 海にはおよばない (7, 15) - 8 characters (beach)

- もとにはおよばない (17, 26) - 9 characters (set expression, "もと" from "足元")
- 技術にはおよびません (9, 19) - 10 characters (polite)

- Pattern includes the preceding noun (full grammatical construction)
- WaOrMoMatcher handles both には and にも variants (set expression uses も)
- OyobuMatcher uses hiragana matching (およば/および) not kanji (及ば/及び)

### ✓ Completed: に言わせれば・に言わせると・に言わせたら (if you ask / according to)

**Grammar Point**: に言わせれば・に言わせると・に言わせたら
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + に + 言わせれば (e.g., 私にいわせれば)
2. Noun + に + 言わせると (e.g., 母にいわせると)
3. Noun + に + 言わせたら (e.g., 先生にいわせたら)
4. Noun + から + 言わせれば (e.g., 私からいわせれば) - から variant

**Meaning**:
- "If you ask (A), (B)" / "According to (A), (B)" / "In (A)'s opinion, (B)"
- Indicates a person or group giving a strong opinion
- Literal: "If (A) is allowed to speak, (B)"
- Combination of に/から (particle) + 言う (to say) + せる (causative auxiliary) + ば/と/たら (conditional)
- Can sound self-important, so best used sparingly when giving your own opinions
- ば is the most common conditional form

**Tokenization patterns**:
**に言わせれば (standard):**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- いわ (動詞/自立, base=いう, 未然形)
- せれ (動詞/接尾, base=せる, 仮定形)
- ば (助詞/接続助詞)

**に言わせると (と conditional):**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- いわ (動詞/自立, base=いう, 未然形)
- せる (動詞/接尾, base=せる, 基本形)
- と (助詞/接続助詞)

**に言わせたら (たら conditional):**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- いわ (動詞/自立, base=いう, 未然形)
- せ (動詞/接尾, base=せる, 連用形)
- たら (助動詞, base=た, 仮定形)

**から variant:**
- Same structure but から (助詞/格助詞/一般) instead of に

**Implementation**:
Created 4 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NiOrKaraMatcher - Matches に or から as 助詞/格助詞/一般
2. IwaMatcher - Matches いわ (base=いう, 未然形, 動詞/自立)
3. SeMatcher - Matches せれ (仮定形), せる (基本形), or せ (連用形) - all base=せる, 動詞/接尾
4. BaOrToOrTaraMatcher - Matches ば (助詞/接続助詞), と (助詞/接続助詞), or たら (助動詞, 仮定形)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- super::noun_matcher() (preceding noun)
- NiOrKaraMatcher (に or から)
- IwaMatcher (いわ)
- SeMatcher (せれ/せる/せ)
- BaOrToOrTaraMatcher (ば/と/たら)

2. test_niiwasereba_kare - 彼にいわせれば (26, 33) - ば form in mid-sentence

6. test_niiwasetara_variant - 先生にいわせたら (0, 8) - たら conditional

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Noun + に/から + いわせれば/と/たら:
- 私にいわせれば (0, 7) - 7 characters (に + ば form)
- 彼にいわせれば (26, 33) - 7 characters (に + ば form)
- 家にいわせれば (2, 9) - 7 characters (に + ば form)
- 私からいわせれば (0, 8) - 8 characters (から + ば form)

- 先生にいわせたら (0, 8) - 8 characters (に + たら form)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding noun (full grammatical construction)
- NiOrKaraMatcher handles both に and から particles (から is less common but valid)

- BaOrToOrTaraMatcher handles all three conditional particles
- All structure variants tested with realistic sentences from grammar_points_data.json
- ば is most common, と and たら are alternative conditionals with similar meaning
- Pattern works with any noun indicating a person or group

**Session highlights**:
- Implemented とは言うものの (although it is said that)
- Set expression combining と (quotative) + は (adverbial) + いう (to say) + ものの (conjunction)

- Pattern appears after verbs, adjectives, and nouns to express "although (A), (B)"

### ✓ Completed: とは言うものの (although it is said that)

**Grammar Point**: とは言うものの
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + とは言うものの (e.g., できるようになるとはいうものの)
2. い-Adjective + とは言うものの (e.g., いいとはいうものの)
3. な-Adjective + だ + とは言うものの (e.g., 便利だというものの)
4. Noun + だ + とは言うものの (e.g., 学生だというものの)
5. Sentence-initial: とは言うものの (continuation from previous sentence - not currently tested)

**Meaning**:
- "Although it is said that (A), (B)" / "Having said that, (B)"
- Acknowledges statement (A) while pointing out important information (B)
- The (B) phrase after ものの is considered the important or defining detail
- Formal expression, seen more often in writing
- Combination of と (quotative particle) + は (adverbial particle) + 言う (to say) + ものの (conjunction meaning "although")

**Tokenization patterns**:
**With は (とはいうものの):**
- と (助詞/格助詞/引用)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- いう (動詞/自立, base=いう, 基本形)
- ものの (助詞/接続助詞)

**Without は (というものの):**
- と (助詞/格助詞/引用)
- いう (動詞/自立, base=いう, 基本形)
- ものの (助詞/接続助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created 4 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. ToMatcher - Matches と as 助詞/格助詞/引用
2. WaMatcher - Matches は as 助詞/係助詞 (optional)
3. IuMatcher - Matches いう (動詞/自立, base=いう)
4. MononoMatcher - Matches ものの (助詞/接続助詞)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding word: verb, adjective, noun, or だ)
- ToMatcher (と)
- Optional(WaMatcher) (は - optional)
- IuMatcher (いう)
- MononoMatcher (ものの)

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_tohaiumonono_verb - なるとはいうものの (16, 25) - verb form with は
2. test_tohaiumonono_i_adjective - いいとはいうものの (12, 21) - i-adjective with は
3. test_tohaiumonono_na_adjective_with_da - だというものの (6, 13) - na-adjective without は
4. test_tohaiumonono_noun_with_da - だというものの (4, 11) - noun without は

**Note**: Sentence-initial usage test is commented out with TODO. The pattern typically appears mid-sentence after a word. Sentence-initial usage would be a continuation from a previous sentence, which is less common and would require different matching logic.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures preceding word + と(は)いうものの:
- なるとはいうものの (16, 25) - 9 characters (verb + pattern with は)
- いいとはいうものの (12, 21) - 9 characters (i-adj + pattern with は)
- だというものの (6, 13) - 7 characters (だ + pattern without は)
- だというものの (4, 11) - 7 characters (だ + pattern without は)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (full grammatical construction)
- Optional は matcher handles both variants (とはいう vs という)
- Priority set to 5 to override the generic "ものの" pattern (priority 1)
- The は is optional in natural speech, especially after だ
- Pattern works with any verb, adjective, or noun
- Emphasizes that the information after ものの is more important

### ✓ Completed: に恥じない (lives up to / not ashamed of)

**Grammar Point**: に恥じない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + に恥（は）じない (standard negative)
2. Noun + に恥（は）じない + Noun (modifying a noun)
3. Noun + に恥（は）じません (polite negative)
4. Noun + に恥（は）じません + Noun (polite, modifying a noun)

**Meaning**:
- "Lives up to (A)" / "Not ashamed of (A)" / "Without shaming (A)"
- Formal expression showing something lives up to expected standard or quality level
- Combination of に (case-marking particle) + 恥じる (る-Verb "to shame") negated form
- Literal translation: "without shaming (A)"
- Usually used with nouns indicating well-known aspects: 名（な）"name", 名誉（めいよ）"reputation"
- Only used in positive contexts where someone/something is being praised

**Tokenization pattern**:
**Standard form (に恥じない):**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- はじ (動詞/自立, base=はじる, 一段, 未然形)
- ない (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ, 基本形)

**Polite form (に恥じません):**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- はじ (動詞/自立, base=はじる, 一段, 連用形)
- ませ (助動詞, 特殊・マス, 未然形)
- ん (助動詞, 不変化型)

**Implementation**:
Created 4 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞/一般
2. HajiMatcher - Matches はじ (base=はじる, 動詞/自立, 未然形 or 連用形)
3. NaiOrMaseMatcher - Matches ない (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ) OR ませ (助動詞, 特殊・マス)
4. NMatcher - Matches ん (助動詞, 不変化型) for polite negative ending

Pattern matcher sequence:
- noun_matcher() (preceding noun)
- NiMatcher (に)
- HajiMatcher (はじ in 未然形 or 連用形)
- NaiOrMaseMatcher (ない or ませ)
- Optional(NMatcher) (ん for polite negative)

- 名にはじない (18, 24) - 6 characters (standard)
- 名にはじない (5, 11) - 6 characters (standard)
- 名にはじない (3, 9) - 6 characters (standard)
- 名にはじません (5, 12) - 7 characters (polite)
- 名にはじません (6, 13) - 7 characters (polite)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding noun (full grammatical construction)
- HajiMatcher handles both 未然形 (for ない) and 連用形 (for ます)
- NaiOrMaseMatcher handles both casual and polite forms
- Optional ん matcher completes polite negative (ません)
- Used only in positive contexts to praise/show respect

- Handles casual (にならない), polite (になりません), and emphatic (にはならない) forms
- Pattern includes full grammatical construction: Noun + とは + くらべもの + に + [は] + なる + ない/ません

### ✓ Completed: とは比べものにならない (cannot be compared to)

**Grammar Point**: とは比べものにならない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + とは比べものにならない (standard form)
2. Noun + とは比べものになりません (polite form)
3. Noun + とは比べものにはならない (with emphatic は)

**Meaning**:
- "Cannot be compared to (A)" / "Nothing compared to (A)"
- Emphasizes that something is far beyond what could possibly be compared
- Combination of と (case-marking particle) + は (adverbial particle) + 比べもの (comparable thing) + に (particle) + ならない (negated なる)
- Used to express that quality, quantity, or some feature is not comparable
- The optional は before ならない adds further emphasis or highlights contrast

**Tokenization pattern**:
**Standard form (にならない):**
- Noun (名詞)
- と (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- くらべ (動詞/自立, base=くらべる, 連用形)
- もの (名詞/接尾/一般)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- なら (動詞/自立 or 非自立, base=なる, 未然形)
- ない (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ)

**Emphatic form (にはならない):**
- Same as above, but with additional は (助詞/係助詞) before なる

**Polite form (になりません):**
- Same structure, but:
- なり (動詞/自立, base=なる, 連用形)
- ませ (助動詞, base=ます, 未然形)
- ん (助動詞, 不変化型)

**Implementation**:
Created 8 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. ToMatcher - Matches と as 助詞/格助詞/一般
2. WaMatcher - Matches は as 助詞/係助詞
3. KurabeMatcher - Matches くらべ (base=くらべる, 動詞)
4. MonoMatcher - Matches もの as 名詞
5. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞/一般
6. NaruMatcher - Matches なら (未然形) or なり (連用形), base=なる
7. NaiOrMaseMatcher - Matches ない (助動詞) or ませ (助動詞)
8. NMatcher - Matches ん (助動詞) for polite negative ending

Pattern matcher sequence:
- noun_matcher() (preceding noun)
- ToMatcher (と)
- WaMatcher (は)
- KurabeMatcher (くらべ)
- MonoMatcher (もの)
- NiMatcher (に)
- Optional(WaMatcher) (optional は before なる for emphasis)
- NaruMatcher (なら/なり)
- NaiOrMaseMatcher (ない/ませ)
- Optional(NMatcher) (ん for polite negative)

4. test_tohakurabemononinaranai_polite - 技術とはくらべものになりません (2, 17) - polite form
5. test_tohakurabemononinaranai_quality - 店とはくらべものにならない (9, 22) - standard form

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Noun + とは比べものにならない/になりません:
- 部品とはくらべものにならない (17, 31) - 14 characters
- スパゲティーとはくらべものにならない (5, 23) - 18 characters
- 北海道とはくらべものにはならない (7, 23) - 16 characters (with は)
- 技術とはくらべものになりません (2, 17) - 15 characters (polite)
- 店とはくらべものにならない (9, 22) - 13 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding noun (full grammatical construction)
- Optional は matcher between に and なる handles emphatic variant
- NaruMatcher accepts both なら (未然形, for ない) and なり (連用形, for ます)
- NaiOrMaseMatcher handles both casual (ない) and polite (ませ) endings
- Optional ん matcher completes polite negative (ません)
- Used to emphasize large differences in quality, quantity, or other measurable features
- Neologism 比べもの = 比べる (compare) + もの (thing) = "comparable thing"

**Session highlights**:

- Formal expression used to emphasize that something is not restricted to a particular case
- Handles both casual (ことではない) and polite (ことではありません) forms

### ✓ Completed: に限ったことではない (not limited to / not only)

**Grammar Point**: に限ったことではない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard/Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + に限（かぎ）ったことではない (standard form)
2. Noun + に限（かぎ）ったことではありません (polite form)

**Meaning**:
- "It is not limited to (A)" / "Not only (A), but (B)" / "Not restricted to (A), (B) too"
- Fairly emphatic expression using 限（かぎ）る "to limit" in past tense form
- Combination of に (case-marking particle) + 限（かぎ）った (past tense of 限る) + こと (thing) + で (conjunctive form of だ) + は (adverbial particle) + ない (adjective)
- Literal translation: "it is not something that is limited to (A)"
- Often indicates that (A) is something that is not especially rare, so should not be treated in any special way
- Reserved for fairly formal situations

**Tokenization pattern**:
**Standard form (ことではない):**
- Noun (名詞/一般 or 名詞/副詞可能)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- かぎっ (動詞/自立, base=かぎる, 連用タ接続)
- た (助動詞, 特殊・タ, 基本形)
- こと (名詞/非自立/一般)
- で (助動詞, 特殊・ダ, 連用形, base=だ)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- ない (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ) or ない (形容詞/自立)

**Polite form (ことではありません):**
- Same as above for tokens 1-7
- あり (動詞/自立, base=ある, 連用形)
- ませ (助動詞, 特殊・マス, 未然形)
- ん (助動詞, 不変化型)

**Implementation**:
Created 9 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞/一般
2. KagitMatcher - Matches かぎっ (base=かぎる, 動詞)
3. TaMatcher - Matches た (助動詞, 特殊・タ)
4. KotoMatcher - Matches こと (名詞)
5. DeMatcher - Matches で (助動詞, base=だ)
6. WaMatcher - Matches は (助詞/係助詞)
7. NaiOrArimasenMatcher - Matches ない (助動詞 or 形容詞) OR あり (動詞, base=ある)
8. MaseMatcher - Optional, matches ませ (助動詞, 特殊・マス)
9. NMatcher - Optional, matches ん (助動詞)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- noun_matcher() (preceding noun)
- NiMatcher (に)
- KagitMatcher (かぎっ)
- TaMatcher (た)
- KotoMatcher (こと)
- DeMatcher (で)
- WaMatcher (は)
- NaiOrArimasenMatcher (ない or あり)
- Optional(MaseMatcher) (ませ for polite)
- Optional(NMatcher) (ん for polite)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_nikagittakotodehanai_young_people - 若者にかぎったことではない (22, 35) - standard form
2. test_nikagittakotodehanai_today - 今日にかぎったことではない (0, 13) - standard form
3. test_nikagittakotodehanai_japanese - 日本語にかぎったことではない (8, 22) - standard form
4. test_nikagittakotodehanai_polite_this_company - 会社にかぎったことではありません (11, 27) - polite form
5. test_nikagittakotodehanai_polite_men - 男性にかぎったことではありません (5, 21) - polite form

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Noun + に限ったことではない/ありません:
- 若者にかぎったことではない (22, 35) - 13 characters
- 今日にかぎったことではない (0, 13) - 13 characters
- 日本語にかぎったことではない (8, 22) - 14 characters
- 会社にかぎったことではありません (11, 27) - 16 characters
- 男性にかぎったことではありません (5, 21) - 16 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding noun (full grammatical construction)
- Handles both casual and polite forms with optional matchers
- NaiOrArimasenMatcher handles both ない endings (standard) and あり (start of ありません polite form)
- Optional matchers for ませ and ん allow matching both standard and polite forms with same matcher sequence
- Used to indicate something is not rare or special, doesn't need special treatment
- Reserved for formal situations

### ✓ Completed: の至り (the utmost / extreme of)

**Grammar Point**: の至り
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + の + 至り + だ (standard form)
2. Noun + の + 至り + です (polite form)

**Meaning**:
- "The utmost (A)" / "The extreme of (A)"
- Formal expression for highlighting that (A) is the highest level of something
- Combination of の (case-marking particle) + 至り (noun meaning "pinnacle" or "climax")
- Usually used with emotion nouns that can be experienced to a high degree
- Common with: 光栄 (honor), 感謝 (gratitude), 恐縮 (obligation), 赤面 (embarrassment)
- Set expression: 若気の至り "youthful indiscretion" (blunder made due to being young)

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Noun (名詞 - various subtypes: 一般, サ変接続, etc.)
- の (助詞/連体化) - case-marking particle
- 至り (名詞/一般, base=至り, surface=至り)
- Optional: だ/です (助動詞) or で (助詞/格助詞/一般)

**Implementation**:
Created 2 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NoMatcher - Matches の as 助詞/連体化
2. ItariMatcher - Matches 至り as 名詞 (base=至り)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- noun_matcher() (preceding noun)
- NoMatcher (の particle)
- ItariMatcher (至り noun)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**

### ✓ Completed: をよそに (ignoring/disregarding)

**Grammar Point**: をよそに
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + をよそに (e.g., 心配をよそに, 反対をよそに, 視線をよそに)
2. Phrase + の + をよそに (e.g., 体調が悪いのをよそに)

**Meaning**:
- "Ignoring (A)" / "Disregarding (A)" / "Putting (A) completely aside, (B)"
- Combination of を (adverbial particle) + 余所（よそ）"other place" + に (case-marking particle)
- Literal meaning: "putting (A) completely aside"
- Expresses that (A) has nothing to do with the speaker or that the speaker is ignoring it in relation to something else
- (A) expresses some overall situation, while (B) indicates an action done without thought or care toward those circumstances
- Often used critically about actions that are seen as irresponsible
- Formal expression, seen more often in writing
- Generally not used to refer to the speaker's own actions

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Word (Noun or の) + を + よそ + に
- Preceding word: Noun, の (名詞/非自立/一般), or other word
- を: 助詞/格助詞/一般
- よそ: 名詞/代名詞/一般 (base=よそ, surface=よそ)
- に: 助詞/格助詞/一般

**Implementation**:
Created 3 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. WoMatcher - Matches を as 助詞/格助詞/一般
2. YosoMatcher - Matches よそ as 名詞 (any noun subtype)
3. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞/一般

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding word - noun, の, or other)
- WoMatcher (を particle)
- YosoMatcher (よそ noun)
- NiMatcher (に particle)

5. test_woyosoni_concerns - 懸念をよそに (7, 13) - concerns context

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures preceding word + をよそに:
- 心配をよそに (4, 10) - 6 characters (noun + pattern)
- 反対をよそに (6, 12) - 6 characters (noun + pattern)
- 視線をよそに (5, 11) - 6 characters (noun + pattern)
- のをよそに (8, 13) - 5 characters (の + pattern)
- 懸念をよそに (7, 13) - 6 characters (noun + pattern)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (full grammatical construction)
- Works with both direct nouns (心配, 反対, 視線, 懸念) and nominalized phrases (の)

**Session highlights**:

### ✓ Completed: ないまでも (even if not / may not be... but)

**Grammar Point**: ないまでも
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[ない] + までも + Phrase (e.g., 言えないまでも)
2. Noun + ではない + までも + Phrase (e.g., 毎週ではないまでも)
3. Noun + じゃない + までも + Phrase (e.g., 幸せじゃないまでも)

**Meaning**:
- "Even though not (A), (B)" / "It may not be (A), but (B)"
- Combination of negated form + まで (case-marking particle "until") + も (adverbial particle "even")
- Presents extreme (A) which hasn't been reached, but lower extent (B) cannot be denied
- Literal: "even though not reaching (A), (B)"
- Used in both positive and negative situations
- (B) phrase often expresses wish/demand with たい, てほしい, てください, etc.

**Tokenization patterns**:
**For Verb + ない + までも:**
- Verb in 未然形 (e.g., 言え, なくせ, なれ)
- ない (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ, 基本形)
- まで (助詞/副助詞)
- も (助詞/係助詞)

**For Noun + ではない + までも:**
- Noun (名詞)
- で (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- ない (形容詞/自立, 形容詞・アウオ段, 基本形) - Note: adjective, not auxiliary
- まで (助詞/副助詞)
- も (助詞/係助詞)

**For Noun + じゃない + までも:**
- Noun (名詞)
- じゃ (助詞/副助詞)
- ない (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ, 基本形)
- まで (助詞/副助詞)
- も (助詞/係助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created compound matcher using wildcard approach:
- VerbNounOrAdjMatcher: Matches verb, noun, or adjective as starting point
- Wildcard(0, 2): Captures optional particles before ない (で+は or じゃ)
- NaimademoCompound: Matches ない (either auxiliary or adjective)
- MadeMatcher: Matches まで particle
- MoMatcher: Matches も particle

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Custom(VerbNounOrAdjMatcher) - Start with verb/noun/adj
- Wildcard with stop_conditions to prevent over-matching
- NaimademoCompound (ない in either form)
- MadeMatcher (まで)
- MoMatcher (も)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_naimademo_verb_negative_masterpiece - 言えないまでも (10, 17) - verb + ない
2. test_naimademo_verb_negative_eliminate - なくせないまでも (8, 16) - verb + ない
3. test_naimademo_verb_negative_become - なれないまでも (8, 15) - verb + ない
4. test_naimademo_noun_dewanai - 毎週ではないまでも (0, 9) - noun + で + は + ない
5. test_naimademo_noun_janai - 幸せじゃないまでも (12, 21) - noun + じゃ + ない

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full construction:
- 言えないまでも (10, 17) - 7 characters (verb + ない + までも)
- なくせないまでも (8, 16) - 8 characters (verb + ない + までも)
- なれないまでも (8, 15) - 7 characters (verb + ない + までも)
- 毎週ではないまでも (0, 9) - 9 characters (noun + で + は + ない + までも)
- 幸せじゃないまでも (12, 21) - 9 characters (noun + じゃ + ない + までも)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (verb or noun) for full grammatical construction
- Wildcard approach handles variable-length particle sequences before ない
- ない can be either auxiliary verb (助動詞) or adjective (形容詞) depending on context
- For verbs: ない is auxiliary, for ではない: ない is adjective, for じゃない: ない is auxiliary
- Starting with VerbNounOrAdjMatcher prevents over-matching from particles earlier in sentence
- Stop conditions in wildcard prevent matching beyond intended scope

### ✓ Completed: に即して (in accordance with / based on)

**Grammar Point**: に即して
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + に即（そく）して (て-form, used before verbs)
2. Noun + に即（そく）した + Noun (た-form, modifying noun)
3. Noun + に則（そく）して/に則（そく）した (alternative kanji variant)

**Meaning**:
- "In accordance with (A)" / "In line with (A)" / "Based on (A)"
- Formal expression for doing something in the best possible way to handle a matter
- Combination of に (case-marking particle) + 即（そく）する (する-verb meaning "to be in accordance with")
- Used when things are being done based on circumstances, situations, facts, laws, or standards
- More formal than similar pattern に沿って
- Alternative kanji 則（そく）has the same meaning and pronunciation

**Tokenization patterns**:
**For に即して (て-form):**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- 即し (動詞/自立, base=即す, 五段・サ行, 連用形)
- て (助詞/接続助詞)

**For に即した (た-form, modifying noun):**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- 即し (動詞/自立, base=即す, 五段・サ行, 連用形)
- た (助動詞, 特殊・タ, 基本形)

**For に則して/に則した (alternative kanji):**
- Same pattern but with 則し (base=則す) instead of 即し (base=即す)

**Implementation**:
Created 3 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞/一般
2. SokushiMatcher - Matches 即し or 則し (both verbs 動詞/自立, base forms: 即す or 則す)
3. TeOrTaMatcher - Matches て (助詞/接続助詞) or た (助動詞, base=た)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- noun_matcher() (preceding noun)
- NiMatcher (に)
- SokushiMatcher (即し or 則し)
- TeOrTaMatcher (て or た)

4. test_nisokushita_modifying_noun_punishment - 法律に即した (20, 26) - た-form with different context
5. test_nisokushite_alternative_kanji - 現実に則した (0, 6) - alternative kanji 則

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Noun + に即して/に即した/に則した:
- ルールに即して (3, 10) - 7 characters (て-form)
- 事実に即して (9, 15) - 6 characters (て-form)
- 法律に即した (0, 6) - 6 characters (た-form)
- 法律に即した (20, 26) - 6 characters (た-form)
- 現実に則した (0, 6) - 6 characters (alternative kanji, た-form)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding noun (full grammatical construction)
- て-form (に即して) is used before verbs or at end of sentence
- た-form (に即した) is used to modify a following noun
- Both kanji variants (即 and 則) have the same meaning and pronunciation

- Used when doing something based on rules, facts, circumstances, or standards

### ✓ Completed: には及ばない① (no need to / unnecessary)

**Grammar Point**: には及ばない①
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + には及ばない (standard)
2. Verb[dictionary] + には及ばない
3. (お/ご) + Noun + には及ばない (polite prefix)
4. Polite forms: には及びません

**Meaning**:
- "There is no need to (A)" / "(A) is unnecessary"
- Combination of に (case-marking particle) + は (topic particle) + 及ばない (negated form of 及ぶ "to reach")
- Literal: "It does not extend to (A)"
- Used after nouns or verbs in dictionary form
- Often preceded by お or ご for politeness in formal situations
- Used in polite speech to decline offers or indicate something isn't necessary

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Preceding word (Noun or Verb in basic form)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- 及び or 及ば (動詞/自立/五段・バ行, base=及ぶ) - 連用形 or 未然形
- ない (助動詞, 特殊・ナイ) OR ませ (助動詞, 特殊・マス, 未然形)
- Optional: ん (助動詞, 不変化型) for polite negative

**Implementation**:
Created 5 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞/一般
2. WaMatcher - Matches は as 助詞/係助詞
3. OyobuMatcher - Matches 及ぶ verb (及び in 連用形 or 及ば in 未然形)
4. NaiMaseMatcher - Matches ない (casual negative) or ませ (polite negative prep)
5. NMatcher - Optional ん for polite negative ending (ません)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (noun/verb)
- NiMatcher (に)
- WaMatcher (は)
- OyobuMatcher (及び/及ば)
- NaiMaseMatcher (ない/ませ)
- Optional(NMatcher) (ん for ません)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**

4. test_niwaoyobanai_o_prefix - お礼には及びません (0, 9) - お prefix (included in pattern)
5. test_niwaoyobanai_explanation - 説明には及びません (15, 24) - する-verb + polite

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures preceding word + には及ばない/には及びません:
- 心配には及ばない (0, 8) - 8 characters (noun + pattern, casual)

- 心配には及びません (1, 10) - 9 characters (after ご prefix)
- お礼には及びません (0, 9) - 9 characters (includes お prefix)
- 説明には及びません (15, 24) - 9 characters (noun + pattern, polite)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (full grammatical construction)
- Handles both casual (ない) and polite (ません) endings
- Optional ん matcher allows matching both ませ and ません
- Often used with お/ご prefixes in polite contexts
- The ① variant means "no need to" (polite decline)

**Session highlights**:
- Implemented を限りに (ending with / no longer than / as of)
- Formal expression for announcing the end of something
- Handles both を限りに and 限りで variants (with and without を particle)
- Pattern includes noun + optional を + かぎり/限り + に/で

### ✓ Completed: を限りに (ending with / no longer than / as of)

**Grammar Point**: を限りに
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + を限（かぎ）りに (formal)
2. Noun + 限（かぎ）りで (less formal variant)

**Meaning**:
- "Ending with (A), (B)" / "No longer than (A), (B)" / "As of (A)"
- Expresses a time for which something that was continuing will finish by
- Used for making proclamations or announcements
- Combination of を (adverbial particle) + 限り (limit) + に/で (case-marking particle)
- Primarily used with words indicating specific time or place viewed temporally
- Exception: Set idiom 声を限りに "to the limit of one's voice" (crying/shouting as loud as possible)

**Tokenization patterns**:
**For を限りに:**
- Noun (名詞/副詞可能 or 名詞/一般)
- を (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- かぎり (名詞/ナイ形容詞語幹, base=かぎり)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)

**For 限りで:**
- Noun (名詞/副詞可能 or 名詞/一般)
- 限り (名詞/非自立/副詞可能, base=限り)
- で (助詞/格助詞/一般)

**Implementation**:
Created 3 custom matchers for flexible pattern matching:
1. WoMatcher - Matches を as 助詞/格助詞/一般 (optional)
2. KagiriMatcher - Matches かぎり (名詞/ナイ形容詞語幹) or 限り (名詞/非自立) with base form check
3. NiOrDeMatcher - Matches に or で as 助詞/格助詞

Pattern matcher sequence:
- super::noun_matcher() (noun)
- TokenMatcher::Optional(WoMatcher) (optional を)
- KagiriMatcher (かぎり or 限り)
- NiOrDeMatcher (に or で)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_wokagirini_store_closing - 明日をかぎりに (0, 7) - time announcement
2. test_wokagirini_moving_abroad - 今月をかぎりに (0, 7) - life change announcement
3. test_wokagirini_concert_disbanding - コンサートをかぎりに (6, 16) - event announcement
4. test_kagirinde_variant - 今年限りで (0, 5) - で variant (less formal)
5. test_wokagirini_voice_idiom - 声をかぎりに (3, 9) - set idiom exception

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Noun + [を] + かぎり/限り + に/で:
- 明日をかぎりに (0, 7) - 7 characters (noun + を + かぎり + に)
- 今月をかぎりに (0, 7) - 7 characters (noun + を + かぎり + に)
- コンサートをかぎりに (6, 16) - 10 characters (compound noun + を + かぎり + に)
- 今年限りで (0, 5) - 5 characters (noun + 限り + で, no を)
- 声をかぎりに (3, 9) - 6 characters (noun + を + かぎり + に, idiom)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding noun (full grammatical construction)
- Optional を matcher allows matching both を限りに and 限りで variants
- KagiriMatcher checks for both かぎり (hiragana) and 限り (kanji) as they tokenize differently
- Base form can be either かぎり or 限り depending on tokenization
- More formal than similar structure 限りで
- Used primarily for announcements: store closings, retirements, disbanding, moving, etc.
- Special idiom: 声を限りに "crying/shouting to the limit of one's voice"

- All structure variants tested with realistic subtitle sentences from grammar_points_data.json

### ✓ Completed: といおうか (how to put it / shall I say)

**Grammar Point**: といおうか
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structure**:
- Any word + といおうか (can be Noun, Verb, い-Adjective, or な-Adjective)
- Typically used in pairs: AといおうかBといおうか

**Meaning**:
- "How to put it" / "How shall I say it" / "Perhaps it's (A), or perhaps it's (B)"
- Combination of と (quotation particle) + 言おう (volitional form of 言う "to say") + か (question particle)
- Used when speaker is uncertain about how to describe something
- Typically appears twice in a sentence as AといおうかBといおうか
- Both (A) and (B) phrase are being questioned by the speaker
- Can be used with almost any word type before the と particle
- More emphatic than the common といおうか, used for literary effect or formal situations
- Often used with vague/imprecise descriptions to avoid but hint at what speaker actually wants to say

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Preceding word (Any - Noun, Verb, Adjective in basic form)
- と (助詞/格助詞/引用) - quotation particle
- いお (動詞/自立, base=いう, 五段・ワ行促音便, 未然ウ接続) - "to say" in volitional prep form
- う (助動詞, 不変化型, 基本形) - volitional auxiliary verb
- か (助詞/副助詞／並立助詞／終助詞) - question particle

**Implementation**:
Created 4 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. ToMatcher - Matches と as 助詞/格助詞/引用 (quotation particle)
2. IoMatcher - Matches いお (verb いう in 未然ウ接続 form)
3. UMatcher - Matches う auxiliary (助動詞, 不変化型)
4. KaMatcher - Matches か particle (助詞/副助詞／並立助詞／終助詞)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding word - can be any type)
- ToMatcher (と quotation particle)
- IoMatcher (いお from いう)
- UMatcher (う auxiliary)
- KaMatcher (か particle)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**

2. test_toiouka_i_adjective_pair - 危ないといおうか (12, 20) - い-adjective + pattern
3. test_toiouka_na_adjective_pair - すぎるといおうか (10, 18) - な-adjective stem + pattern
4. test_toiouka_noun_pair - いとこといおうか (2, 10) - noun + pattern
5. test_toiouka_mixed_types - ないといおうか (7, 14) - mixed types in one sentence

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures preceding word + といおうか:
- いるといおうか (4, 11) - 7 characters (verb + pattern)
- 危ないといおうか (12, 20) - 8 characters (adjective + pattern)
- すぎるといおうか (10, 18) - 8 characters (verb + pattern)
- いとこといおうか (2, 10) - 8 characters (noun + pattern)
- ないといおうか (7, 14) - 7 characters (verb negative + pattern)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (full grammatical construction)
- Each occurrence of といおうか is detected separately (not as a paired pattern)
- Works with all major word types: verbs, い-adjectives, な-adjectives, nouns
- The pattern is typically used twice in a sentence (AといおうかBといおうか) but each is detected independently
- More formal/literary than the common といおうか variant
- When multiple patterns exist in a sentence, they may be returned in non-sequential order

### ✓ Completed: にしたところで (even if / even though)

**Grammar Point**: にしたところで
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + にしたところで (even in the case of noun)
2. Noun + にしたって (casual variant)
3. Verb/Adj + としたところで (quotation form)
4. Verb/Adj + としたって (casual quotation form)

**Meaning**:
- "Even if (A)" / "Even in the case of (A), (B)"
- Indicates hypothetical situation of (A) from which result (B) will not change
- Expresses lack of power over ultimate result, or lack of significant difference between (A) and other choices
- Combination of に/と (particle) + した (past of する) + ところで (situation marker + で)
- に is used after nouns as case-marking particle
- と is used after verbs/adjectives as quotation particle
- Casual form uses たって instead of たところで

**Tokenization patterns**:
**For にしたところで:**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- し (動詞/自立, base=する, 連用形)
- た (助動詞, base=た)
- ところ (名詞/非自立/副詞可能)
- で (助詞/格助詞/一般)

**For にしたって:**
- Noun (名詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- し (動詞/自立, base=する, 連用形)
- た (助動詞, base=た)
- って (助詞/格助詞/連語)

**For としたところで/としたって:**
- Verb/Adjective (any)
- と (助詞/格助詞/引用) - quotation particle
- し (動詞/自立, base=する, 連用形)
- た (助動詞, base=た)
- ところで or って (particle)

**Implementation**:
Created 5 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NiToMatcher - Matches に or と as 助詞/格助詞
2. ShiMatcher - Matches し (from する, 連用形)
3. TaMatcher - Matches た (auxiliary, base=た)
4. TokorodeTatteMatcher - Matches ところ (名詞/非自立) or って (助詞/格助詞/連語)
5. DeMatcher - Matches で (optional, only for ところで)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding word)
- NiToMatcher (に or と)
- ShiMatcher (し from する)
- TaMatcher (た)
- TokorodeTatteMatcher (ところ or って)
- Optional(DeMatcher) (で only needed for ところで variant)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**

2. test_nishitatokorode_laptop - パソコンにしたところで (3, 14) - noun + にしたところで
3. test_nishitatte_casual - 私にしたって (0, 6) - noun + にしたって (casual)
4. test_toshitatokorode_verb_phrase - だとしたところで (5, 13) - verb + としたところで
5. test_toshitatte_casual - たとしたって (2, 8) - verb + としたって (casual)

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full construction from preceding word through final particle:
- さんにしたところで (5, 14) - 9 characters
- パソコンにしたところで (3, 14) - 11 characters
- 私にしたって (0, 6) - 6 characters
- だとしたところで (5, 13) - 8 characters
- たとしたって (2, 8) - 6 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (noun/verb/adjective) before に/と
- Handles both formal (たところで) and casual (たって) variants
- に is used after nouns (case-marking), と after verbs/adjectives (quotation)
- Optional matcher for で allows matching both ところで and って endings
- All 4 structure variants from grammar_points_data.json tested and working

### ✓ Completed: びる (seeming/looking like)

**Grammar Point**: びる
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + びる (e.g., 大人びる - adult-like)
2. い-Adjective stem + びる (e.g., 幼びる - young-looking, but not recognized by Kagome)
3. Noun/Adj + びた + Noun (modifying noun form, e.g., 古びたアルバム - aged album)
4. Various conjugations: びて, びた, びている

**Meaning**:
- "(A) looking" / "(A) seeming" / "behaving like (A)"
- Used exclusively as a suffix attached to limited set of expressions
- Based on visual information or appearance
- Formal expression, often in literary contexts
- Common expressions: 大人びる (mature-looking), 古びる (aged/weathered), ひなびる (rustic/quaint), 田舎びる (rural-like), 物寂びる (lonely/desolate-looking)

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Single compound verb token: 動詞/自立 with base_form ending in びる
- Examples: surface='大人び' base='大人びる', surface='古び' base='古びる', surface='田舎び' base='田舎びる'
- Auxiliary た is included in pattern range (助動詞)
- Particle て is NOT included in pattern range (助詞/接続助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created BiruVerbMatcher custom matcher:
- Matches: 動詞/自立 with base_form ending in びる
- Excludes bare びる (if it exists)
- Compound form only (unlike めく which can be split)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Custom(Arc::new(BiruVerbMatcher))
- Captures just the verb token, pattern matcher extends range to include auxiliaries

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_biru_otonaびる_て_form - 大人び (3, 6) - adult-looking (て-form, particle not included)
2. test_biru_furuびた_modifying_noun - 古びた (11, 14) - aged (た auxiliary included)
3. test_biru_inakabita_modifying_noun - 田舎びた (12, 16) - rural-like (た auxiliary included)
4. test_biru_hinabita_modifying_noun - ひなびた (0, 4) - rustic/quaint (た auxiliary included)
5. test_biru_inakaびて_いる - 田舎び (4, 7) - rural-like progressive (て particle not included)

**Character Ranges**:
- With た (auxiliary): Includes verb + た (e.g., 古びた, 田舎びた, ひなびた)
- With て (particle): Only verb (e.g., 大人び, 田舎び) - て is separate

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern range behavior: auxiliaries (助動詞) included, particles (助詞) excluded
- 幼びる (from 幼い) is grammatically valid but NOT recognized by Kagome - tokenizes as separate words
- Unlike めく which can have split forms, びる only appears as compound verbs in Kagome's dictionary
- Common with nouns describing appearance or character traits
- Limited set of expressions - not freely productive

### ✓ Completed: とあれば (if/when it comes to)

**Grammar Point**: とあれば
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + とあれば (basic case)
2. Verb + とあれば (quotation form)
3. Adjective + とあれば (quotation form)

**Meaning**:
- "If (A) is the case, then (B)" / "Should (A) be true, then (B)"
- Emphatic version of なら 'if' used in formal situations or for literary effect
- Combination of と (case-marking particle/quotation) + あれば (hypothetical form of ある)
- Used when (A) is a special circumstance leading to (B)
- (B) usually indicates something unavoidable (with structures like しかない, なければならない, ざるを得ない)
- The と particle can be a case marker (after nouns) or quotation marker (after verbs/adjectives)

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Preceding word (Noun, Verb, or Adjective in basic form)
- と (助詞/格助詞/一般 or 助詞/格助詞/引用)
- あれ (動詞/自立, base=ある, 仮定形)
- ば (助詞/接続助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created 3 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. ToMatcher - Matches と as 助詞/格助詞 (handles both 一般 and 引用)
2. AreMatcher - Matches あれ as 仮定形 of ある (五段・ラ行)
3. BaMatcher - Matches ば as 助詞/接続助詞

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any (preceding noun/verb/adjective) + ToMatcher + AreMatcher + BaMatcher
- Captures full grammatical construction from preceding word through ば

### ✓ Completed 2: ときたら (when it comes to / that darn)

**Grammar Point**: ときたら
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Casual

**Pattern structure**:
- Noun + ときたら

**Meaning**:
- "When it comes to (A)" / "That darn (A)" / "That blasted (A)"
- Casually expressing mild frustration toward (A) in a lighthearted manner
- Can also highlight something about (A) that is "to be expected" or "only natural"
- Combination of と (case-marking particle) + 来る (to come) + たら (conditional)
- Almost exclusively written in kana (ときたら), not kanji (と来たら)
- Literal: "when (A) comes" → "when it comes to (A)"
- The context (B clause) determines whether it's frustration or just highlighting expectations

**Tokenization pattern** (how Kagome processes it):
**Important**: Despite the grammatical origin being と + 来たら, Kagome tokenizes ときたら differently:
- Noun (名詞/一般)
- とき (動詞/非自立, base=とく, 連用形) - tokenized as verb とく (to sharpen), NOT と + き
- たら (助動詞, base=た, 仮定形)

**Implementation**:
Created 2 custom matchers to match the actual tokenization:
1. TokiMatcher - Matches とき as 動詞/非自立 with base=とく in 連用形
2. TaraMatcher - Matches たら as 助動詞 with base=た in 仮定形

Pattern matcher sequence:
- noun_matcher() + TokiMatcher + TaraMatcher
- Captures full grammatical construction from noun through たら

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_tokitara_son_complaint - 息子ときたら (3, 9) - expressing frustration
2. test_tokitara_brother_complaint - 弟ときたら (3, 8) - expressing frustration
3. test_tokitara_boss_complaint - 上司ときたら (2, 8) - expressing frustration
4. test_tokitara_dog_expected - 犬ときたら (3, 8) - highlighting expected behavior

- 息子ときたら (3, 9) - 6 characters
- 弟ときたら (3, 8) - 5 characters
- 上司ときたら (2, 8) - 6 characters
- 犬ときたら (3, 8) - 5 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding noun (full grammatical construction)
- Almost exclusively written in kana in casual speech/writing
- Kagome's tokenization treats it as とく verb + たら, not と + 来たら
- Dual nuance: frustration/complaint OR highlighting natural expectations
- The (B) clause context determines the nuance (negative → frustration, neutral → expected)
- Common with family members, pets, superiors (うちの息子, うちの犬, 私の上司)

### ✓ Completed: というところ (I would say about / approximately)

**Grammar Point**: というところ
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Phrase + というところ + だ (standard)
2. Phrase + といったところ (past variant)
3. Phrase + というところ + です (polite)
4. Phrase + というところ (without auxiliary)

**Meaning**:
- "I would say about (A)" / "I guess about (A)" / "Approximately (A)"
- Used when highlighting an approximate number or degree for something
- Combination of と (case-marking particle) + いう (to say) + ところ (place/situation)
- Literal: "To be at the point where saying (A) is accurate"
- Most commonly appears following verbs or nouns, occasionally adjectives
- Typically appears at end of sentences with だ or です
- Tends to be used when the number given is the theoretical maximum (not minimum)

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Preceding phrase/number/expression (Any)
- という or といった (助詞/格助詞/連語)
- ところ (名詞/非自立/副詞可能)
- Optional: だ or です (助動詞)

**Implementation**:
Created 3 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. ToiuVariantMatcher - Matches という or といった as 助詞/格助詞/連語
2. TokoroMatcher - Matches ところ as 名詞/非自立/副詞可能
3. DaDeSuMatcher - Matches だ or です as auxiliary verb (optional)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::Any + ToiuVariantMatcher + TokoroMatcher + Optional(DaDeSuMatcher)
- Captures full grammatical construction from preceding word through optional ending

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_toiutokoro_time_period - 終わるというところだ (18, 28) - time duration with だ
2. test_toiutokoro_degree - 辛いというところ (21, 29) - degree/intensity without auxiliary
3. test_toiutokoro_duration - 分というところです (20, 29) - time with です (polite)
4. test_toittatokoro_price - 万といったところです (16, 26) - price with といった variant
5. test_toittatokoro_walk - 時間といったところです (5, 16) - walk time with といった

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures preceding word + という/といった + ところ + optional ending:
- 終わるというところだ (18, 28) - 10 characters (verb + pattern + だ)
- 辛いというところ (21, 29) - 8 characters (adj + pattern, no auxiliary)
- 分というところです (20, 29) - 9 characters (noun + pattern + です)
- 万といったところです (16, 26) - 10 characters (noun + といった + pattern + です)
- 時間といったところです (5, 16) - 11 characters (noun + といった + pattern + です)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (full grammatical construction)
- という and といった both match (present and past forms have same meaning)
- Optional ending (だ/です) allows matching both with and without auxiliary
- Commonly used with numbers, time expressions, and degree descriptions
- Tends to present theoretical maximum rather than minimum approximation

### ✓ Completed 2: に照らして・に照らすと (in light of / in accordance with)

**Grammar Point**: に照らして・に照らすと
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + に照らして (in light of / in accordance with)
2. Noun + に照らした + Noun (modifying noun form)
3. Noun + に照らすと (conditional: if we apply...)

**Meaning**:
- "In light of (A)" / "In accordance with (A)" / "As reflected by (A)"
- Based on standard meaning of う-Verb 照らす ("to illuminate")
- Used comparatively similar to "in light of" in English
- Illustrates (B) from the perspective of (A)
- Usually refers to situations, level of experience, laws, statistics, etc.
- に marks (A) as the "doer" of 照らす (transitive verb)
- Literal: "(B) as reflected/illuminated by (A)"
- More explicitly: "as made clear by (A), (B)" or "as made evident by (A), (B)"
- (B) is almost always a judgment logically based on (A)

**Tokenization patterns**:
**For に照らして:**
- Noun (名詞, e.g., 経験, 法律)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- 照らし (動詞/自立/五段・サ行/連用形, base=照らす)
- て (助詞/接続助詞)

**For に照らした (modifying noun):**
- Noun (名詞, e.g., 統計)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- 照らし (動詞/自立/五段・サ行/連用形, base=照らす)
- た (助動詞/特殊・タ/基本形)

**For に照らすと:**
- Noun (名詞, e.g., 法律)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- 照らす (動詞/自立/五段・サ行/基本形, base=照らす)
- と (助詞/接続助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created 5 custom matchers for flexible pattern matching:
1. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞
2. TerasuVerbMatcher - Matches 照らす as 動詞/自立 (any conjugation)
3. TerasuEndingMatcher - Combined matcher for て, た, or と endings
   - て (助詞/接続助詞) for に照らして
   - た (助動詞) for に照らした
   - と (助詞/接続助詞) for に照らすと

Pattern matcher sequence:
- super::noun_matcher() + NiMatcher + TerasuVerbMatcher + TerasuEndingMatcher
- Captures full grammatical construction from noun through ending

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_niterashite_experience - 経験に照らして (5, 12) - based on experience
2. test_niterashite_law - 法律に照らして (13, 20) - in accordance with law
3. test_niterashita_modifying_noun - 統計に照らした (2, 9) - modifying noun form
4. test_niterasuto_conditional - 法律に照らすと (0, 7) - conditional form

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Noun + に + 照らす + ending:
- 経験に照らして (5, 12) - 7 characters (noun + に + verb + て)
- 法律に照らして (13, 20) - 7 characters (noun + に + verb + て)
- 統計に照らした (2, 9) - 7 characters (noun + に + verb + た)
- 法律に照らすと (0, 7) - 7 characters (noun + に + verb + と)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding noun (full grammatical construction)
- Handles all three main forms: に照らして, に照らした, に照らすと
- に marks the noun as the agent doing the "illuminating" (transitive verb)
- Commonly used with experience (経験), laws (法律), statistics (統計)
- Used to make judgments based on established facts or standards
- More formal expression, often in legal, academic, or professional contexts

### ✓ Completed: や否や (as soon as)

**Grammar Point**: や否や
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structure**:
- Verb[る/dictionary form] + や否（いな）や + Phrase[た]

**Meaning**:
- "As soon as (A)" / "No sooner than (A)"
- Formal expression indicating that (B) happens immediately after (A)
- Literal: "at a point when neither (A) or not (A) could be determined, (B)"
- や highlights that the preceding action is not limited to that point
- Generally used when (B) is something spontaneous or surprising
- Note: Simplified や form (without 否や) also exists with same meaning

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Verb (動詞/自立, conjugation form = 基本形)
- やいなや (助詞/接続助詞, surface=やいなや, base=やいなや)

**Implementation**:
Created YainayaMatcher for precise POS checking:
- Matches やいなや as 助詞/接続助詞
- Surface must be "やいなや"
- POS must be 助詞 (particle) and 接続助詞 (connective particle)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- TokenMatcher::verb_with_form("基本形") + YainayaMatcher
- Captures Verb[dictionary form] + やいなや

- 見るやいなや (12, 18) - 6 characters
- 見るやいなや (4, 10) - 6 characters
- 座るやいなや (4, 10) - 6 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the verb before やいなや (full grammatical construction)
- やいなや is a single token (not split into や + いなや)
- Verb must be in 基本形 (dictionary form/non-past)
- Simplified form や (without いなや) exists but left for future implementation
- Formal expression typically used in literary or formal contexts

### ✓ Completed: にもほどがある (there is a limit to)

**Grammar Point**: にもほどがある
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + にもほどがある (飲み過ぎにもほどがある)
2. い-Adjective + にも + ほどがある (図々しいにもほどがある)
3. な-Adjective + にも + ほどがある (失礼にもほどがある)
4. Noun + にも + ほどがある (冗談にもほどがある)
5. Polite forms: All above + ます (失礼にもほどがあります)

**Meaning**:
- "There is a limit to (A)" / "How (A) can one be" / "You are too (A)"
- Critical expression stating something is excessive or too much based on speaker's opinion
- Component parts: に (particle) + も (adverbial particle) + ほど (noun: bounds/limits) + が (particle) + ある (to exist)
- Literal: "there are bounds even within (A)" → "there is a limit to (A)"
- Usually used critically, but can be used jokingly with positive adjectives (うまいにもほどがある)

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Preceding word (Verb, Adjective, or Noun)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- も (助詞/係助詞)
- ほど (助詞/副助詞)
- が (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- ある or あり (動詞/自立, base=ある)
- Optional: ます (助動詞) for polite form

**Implementation**:
Created 6 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞
2. MoMatcher - Matches も as 助詞/係助詞
3. HodoMatcher - Matches ほど as 助詞/副助詞
4. GaMatcher - Matches が as 助詞/格助詞
5. AruMatcher - Matches ある/あり (verb, base=ある)
6. MasuMatcher - Matches ます auxiliary (optional for polite form)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- Any (preceding word) + に + も + ほど + が + ある + Optional(ます)
- Captures full grammatical construction from preceding word through ある/あります

**Tests Written: 6 tests (all passing)**
1. test_nimohodogaaru_verb - 過ぎにもほどがある (20, 29) - verb form
2. test_nimohodogaaru_i_adjective - 図々しいにもほどがある (14, 25) - い-adjective
3. test_nimohodogaaru_na_adjective - 失礼にもほどがある (17, 26) - な-adjective
4. test_nimohodogaaru_noun - 冗談にもほどがある (16, 25) - noun form
5. test_nimohodogaaru_positive_use - うまいにもほどがある (15, 27) - positive/joking use
6. test_nimohodogaaru_polite - 失礼にもほどがあります (6, 17) - polite form

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures preceding word + にもほどがある:
- 過ぎにもほどがある (20, 29) - 9 characters
- 図々しいにもほどがある (14, 25) - 11 characters
- 失礼にもほどがある (17, 26) - 9 characters
- 冗談にもほどがある (16, 25) - 9 characters
- うまいにもほどがあるだろ (15, 27) - 12 characters (includes だろ)
- 失礼にもほどがあります (6, 17) - 11 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (verb/adjective/noun) before に
- Works with all major word types: verbs, い-adjectives, な-adjectives, nouns
- For compound verbs (飲み過ぎ), pattern starts from the final verb component (過ぎ)
- Despite negative critical usage in most contexts, can be used positively/jokingly (うまいにもほどがある)
- Polite form uses あり+ます (ある conjugated to 連用形)

### ✓ Completed 1: を余儀なくさせる (force/compel to)
**Original session summary for を余儀なくさせる:**
- Implemented を余儀なくさせる (force/compel to do)
- Formal causative pattern combining を余儀ない (unavoidable/inevitable) + させる (causative)
- Handles both Noun + を余儀なくさせる and Verb + こと + を余儀なくさせる
- Works with both standard (させた) and polite (させます) forms
