### ✓ Completed: を余儀なくさせる (force/compel to)

**Grammar Point**: を余儀なくさせる
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard (formal)

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + を余儀なくさせる (standard)
2. Verb + こと + を余儀なくさせる (standard)
3. Noun + を余儀なくさせます (polite)
4. Verb + こと + を余儀なくさせます (polite)

**Meaning**:
- "To force/compel (someone) to do (A)" / "To make (A) unavoidable"
- Formal grammar pattern with same base as を余儀なくされる (passive)
- Uses causative させる instead of passive される
- Component parts: を (particle) + 余儀 (another method) + なく (negative て-form of ない) + させる (causative of する)
- Literally: "to not give another way than to (A)" = "to force (A)"
- Subject is usually a non-human cause (circumstances, situations, events)
- More formal than regular causative expressions

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Noun (名詞, can be followed by こと in verb nominalization)
- を (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- 余儀なく (形容詞/自立, base=余儀ない, 連用テ接続)
- さ (動詞/自立, base=する, 未然レル接続)
- せ (動詞/接尾, base=せる, 一段, 連用形)
- た/ます/る (auxiliary or verb ending)

**Implementation**:
Created 5 custom matchers for precise pattern matching:
1. WoMatcher - Matches を as 助詞/格助詞
2. YoginakuMatcher - Matches 余儀なく (base=余儀ない)
3. SaMatcher - Matches さ (from する, 未然レル接続)
4. SeMatcher - Matches せ (auxiliary verb せる, 動詞/接尾)
5. RuMatcher - Matches る from させる (unused but defined)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- noun_matcher() + を + 余儀なく + さ + せ + Any (for た/ます/る)
- Captures full grammatical construction from noun/こと through conjugated form

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_woyoginakusaseru_noun_standard - 入院を余儀なくさせた (9, 19) - noun + standard form
2. test_woyoginakusaseru_heiten - 閉店を余儀なくさせた (12, 22) - noun + standard form
3. test_woyoginakusaseru_verb_koto - ことを余儀なくさせた (13, 23) - verb + こと + standard
4. test_woyoginakusaseru_polite - 倒産を余儀なくさせます (8, 19) - noun + polite form

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Noun + を余儀なくさせる (full construction):
- 入院を余儀なくさせた (9, 19) - 10 characters
- 閉店を余儀なくさせた (12, 22) - 10 characters
- ことを余儀なくさせた (13, 23) - 10 characters (starts from こと)
- 倒産を余儀なくさせます (8, 19) - 11 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the noun/こと before を (full grammatical construction)
- Works for both verb nominalizations (中止すること) and regular nouns (入院)
- Subject is typically non-human cause: 不注意 (carelessness), 要請 (request), 不足 (shortage)
- Causative construction: さ (未然形 of する) + せ (causative auxiliary せる)
- Related to を余儀なくされる (passive version) - same base, different voice
- 余儀ない is い-adjective meaning "unavoidable" or "inevitable"

### ✓ Completed 2: てはかなわない (can't stand / unbearable)

**Grammar Point**: てはかなわない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[て] + は + かなわない (standard verb form)
2. Verb[られる][て] + は + かなわない (passive - being subjected to action)
3. い-Adj[く] + ては + かなわない (い-adjective)
4. な-Adj/Noun + では + かなわない (な-adjective or noun)

**Meaning**:
- "It's unbearable when (A)" / "I can't stand it when (A)" / "It'd be terrible if (A)"
- Conveys that something is undesirable or intolerable
- Component parts: て/で (conjunctive) + は (adverbial particle) + かなわない (negative of 叶う "to grant/realize")
- Literal translation: "it's not a dream come true for (A)" → "it's unbearable when (A)"
- Often used with passive verbs to indicate dislike of being the target/victim of an action
- CAUTION: かなわない here is from 叶う (to grant), NOT 敵う (to match) - different meanings!

**Tokenization patterns**:
**For Verb[て] + は + かなわない:**
- Verb (動詞/連用形) + て (助詞/接続助詞)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- かなわ (動詞/自立, base=かなう, 未然形)
- ない (助動詞/特殊・ナイ)

**For い-Adj[く] + ては + かなわない:**
- Adjective[く] (形容詞/自立, 連用テ接続) + て (助詞/接続助詞)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- かなわ + ない

**For な-Adj/Noun + では + かなわない:**
- な-Adj/Noun (名詞/形容動詞語幹) + で (助動詞, base=だ, 連用形)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- かなわ + ない

**Implementation**:
Created 4 custom matchers for flexible pattern matching:
1. TeOrDeMatcher - Matches て (助詞/接続助詞) OR で (助動詞 from だ)
2. WaMatcher - Matches は as 助詞/係助詞
3. KanawaMatcher - Matches かなわ (base=かなう, 未然形)
4. NaiMatcher - Matches ない as auxiliary

Pattern matcher sequence:
- Any (verb/adj/noun before て/で) + て/で + は + かなわ + ない
- Captures full grammatical construction

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_tehakanawanai_verb_te - 忘れてはかなわない (6, 15) - regular verb
2. test_tehakanawanai_passive - れてはかなわない (7, 15) - passive verb (折られ...)
3. test_tehakanawanai_i_adj - 痛くてはかなわない (4, 13) - い-adjective
4. test_tehakanawanai_na_adj - あやふやではかなわない (2, 13) - な-adjective
5. test_tehakanawanai_verb_passive_quit - られてはかなわない (13, 22) - passive verb (やめられ...)

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures word + てはかなわない or + ではかなわない:
- 忘れてはかなわない (6, 15) - 9 characters (verb + pattern)
- れてはかなわない (7, 15) - 8 characters (passive auxiliary + pattern)
- 痛くてはかなわない (4, 13) - 9 characters (adj + pattern)
- あやふやではかなわない (2, 13) - 11 characters (な-adj + pattern)
- られてはかなわない (13, 22) - 9 characters (passive auxiliary + pattern)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern starts from the te-form connector (て/で), not always from the main verb stem
- For passive verbs, the pattern captures from the られる/れる auxiliary, not the main verb
- Works with all three major word types: verbs, い-adjectives, な-adjectives/nouns
- て vs で distinction: て after verbs/い-adj, で after な-adj/nouns
- Common with passive verbs to express dislike of being affected by others' actions
- 叶う (grant/realize) vs 敵う (match) - completely different verbs, don't confuse!

### ✓ Completed: ったらない・といったらない (too X for words / indescribably X)

**Grammar Point**: ったらない・といったらない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
- Noun/Adj/Verb + (と)いったら + ない
- Note: The "ったらない" in the pattern name refers to dropping the と particle, NOT a different verb

**Meaning**:
- "There are no words to express (A)" / "Too (A) for words"
- Used when something is at such an extreme level that even (A) is insufficient to describe it
- Emphasizes that the degree or intensity is beyond description
- Can be used in both positive contexts (praise) or negative contexts (complaints)
- Literally: と (quotation) + いう (to say) + たら (conditional) + ない (not exist)
- Underlying meaning: "if one were to say (A), it's not (enough)" → "indescribably (A)"

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Preceding word (Noun, Adjective, or Verb in basic form)
- と (助詞/格助詞/引用) - OPTIONAL
- いっ (動詞/自立/五段・ワ行促音便/連用タ接続, base=いう)
- たら (助動詞/特殊・タ/仮定形, base=た)
- ない (形容詞/自立 or 助動詞/特殊・ナイ)

**Implementation**:
Created 4 custom matchers for precise POS checking:
1. ToParticleMatcher - Matches と as 助詞/格助詞/引用 (optional)
2. IttaMatcher - Matches いっ (連用タ接続 of いう)
3. TaraMatcher - Matches たら (仮定形 of た auxiliary)
4. NaiEndingMatcher - Matches ない (adjective or auxiliary)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- Any (preceding word) + Optional(と) + いっ + たら + ない
- Captures full grammatical construction including the preceding noun/adj/verb

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_toittaranai_noun - 達成感といったらない (12, 20) - noun + pattern
2. test_toittaranai_i_adj - うるさいといったらない (9, 20) - い-adjective + pattern
3. test_toittaranai_i_adj_sa - 優しさといったらない (6, 14) - い-adj with さ suffix
4. test_toittaranai_na_adj - 心配といったらない (13, 22) - な-adjective + pattern

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures preceding word + (と) + いったら + ない:
- 感といったらない (12, 20) - 8 characters (noun ending + pattern)
- うるさいといったらない (9, 20) - 11 characters (full い-adj + pattern)
- さといったらない (6, 14) - 8 characters (さ suffix + pattern)
- 心配といったらない (13, 22) - 9 characters (な-adj + pattern)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the preceding word (full grammatical construction)
- Optional と particle allows matching both といったらない and いったらない forms
- The っ in "ったらない" is from dropping と, not from a different verb (っく)
- い-adjectives often use さ suffix to emphasize the quality/amount being described
- Polite variants (ありません, ありゃしない) not yet implemented but can be added later
- Pattern creates duplicate matches at different confidence levels (with/without preceding word)

- Pattern appears TWICE in succession: Noun A といわず + Noun B といわず

### ✓ Completed: といわず (not just...but also everything)

**Grammar Point**: といわず
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structure**:
- Noun (A) + といわず + Noun (B) + といわず

**Meaning**:
- "Not just (A) or (B), but all" / "(A), (B) and everything else"
- Indicates something applies to everything in a category, not limited to (A) or (B)
- Literal: "without saying (A), and without saying (B), (C)" = "without limiting it to (A)"
- Both (A) and (B) must be part of a logical group (e.g., hands/feet, morning/afternoon, wardrobe/shelves)
- Rarely used in everyday speech - formal/literary expression

**Tokenization pattern**:
The pattern appears twice in succession:
1. Noun (名詞, can be compound like 仕事中)
2. と (助詞/格助詞/引用)
3. いわ (動詞/自立/五段・ワ行促音便/未然形, base=いう)
4. ず (助動詞/特殊・ヌ/連用ニ接続, base=ぬ)
5. 、(comma, optional)
6. Noun (second noun)
7. と (助詞/格助詞/引用)
8. いわ (動詞/自立/五段・ワ行促音便/未然形, base=いう)
9. ず (助動詞/特殊・ヌ/連用ニ接続, base=ぬ)

**Implementation**:
Created 4 custom matchers for precise matching:
1. ToParticleMatcher - Matches と as 助詞/格助詞/引用
2. IwaMatcher - Matches いわ (未然形 of いう)
3. ZuMatcher - Matches ず (助動詞, base=ぬ)
4. CommaMatcher - Matches 、(comma) as 記号/読点
5. CompoundNounMatcher - Matches any noun token (allows compound nouns)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- Noun(s) + と + いわ + ず + (optional comma) + Noun(s) + と + いわ + ず
- Uses CompoundNounMatcher with 0-2 optional additional noun tokens to handle compound nouns like 仕事中

**Tests Written: 3 tests (all passing)**
1. test_toiwazu_tansu_tana - タンスといわず、棚といわず (11, 24) - wardrobe and shelves
2. test_toiwazu_shigotochu_kyuukeichu - 仕事中といわず、休憩中といわず (2, 17) - work/break time (compound nouns)
3. test_toiwazu_kodomo_otona - 子供といわず、大人といわず (5, 18) - children and adults

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full double occurrence (Noun A といわず + Noun B といわず):
- タンスといわず、棚といわず (11, 24) - 13 characters
- 仕事中といわず、休憩中といわず (2, 17) - 15 characters (compound nouns)
- 子供といわず、大人といわず (5, 18) - 13 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes BOTH occurrences of といわず (not just one)
- Handles compound nouns where a noun phrase consists of multiple noun tokens
- Optional comma matcher allows for punctuation between the two occurrences
- Must be used with nouns from the same logical category/group
- Classical grammar element (ず from ぬ) expressing negation

**Session highlights**:
- Implemented めく・めいた (shows signs of, has appearance of)
- Handles TWO distinct tokenization patterns: split (Noun + めく) and compound (春めく, 夏めく)
- Split form: Noun + めく(動詞/非自立) - e.g., 謎めく, 冗談めく, 皮肉めく

### ✓ Completed: めく・めいた (shows signs of, has appearance of)

**Grammar Point**: めく・めいた
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + めく/めいて/めいている/めいた (split form)
2. Compound verb: 春めく/夏めく/冬めく as single or split token

**Meaning**:
- "Shows signs of (A)" / "Has the appearance of (A)"
- Unique う-verb suffix that attaches to nouns
- Expresses speaker's perception that something appears to be (A)
- The thing described usually isn't actually (A), just appears that way
- Used with set nouns: 謎めく (mysterious), 皮肉めく (sarcastic), season names

**Tokenization patterns**:
**Split form** (most common):
- Noun (名詞, e.g., 謎, 冗談, 皮肉, 冬)
- めく (動詞/非自立, base=めく, 連用タ接続)

**Compound form** (less common, mainly seasons):
- Single verb token with base ending in めく (動詞/自立, e.g., base=春めく, 夏めく)
- Interesting: 冬めく tokenizes as SPLIT, while 春めく and 夏めく tokenize as COMPOUND

**Implementation**:
Created MekuVerbMatcher that handles both patterns:
- Matches めく as 動詞/非自立 (split form)
- Matches verbs with base ending in めく as 動詞/自立 (compound form)
- Optional noun matcher allows pattern to match both forms

Pattern matcher sequence:
- Optional(noun_matcher()) + MekuVerbMatcher
- For split: captures Noun + めく (e.g., 謎めい, 冗談めいた)
- For compound: captures just verb token (e.g., 春めい, 夏めいて)

**Tests Written: 8 tests (all passing)**
1. test_meku_meite_compound - 春めいてきた (20, 23) - compound verb form
2. test_meku_meite_split - 冗談めいて (0, 4) - split form
3. test_meku_meita_modifying_noun - 皮肉めいた (0, 5) - past form modifying noun
4. test_meku_meita_mysterious - 謎めいた (6, 10) - common expression
5. test_meku_season_compound - 夏めいてきた (11, 14) - compound season verb
6. test_meku_meiteimasu_polite_compound - 春めいています (6, 9) - polite compound
7. test_meku_meitekimashita_polite_split - 冬めいてきました (9, 12) - polite split (冬 is split!)
8. test_meku_nazo_meiteiru - 謎めいている (9, 12) - progressive form

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures appropriate range based on tokenization:
- Split form: Noun + めく (e.g., 謎めいた 6-10, 冗談めい 0-4, 冬めい 9-12)
- Compound form: Just verb token (e.g., 春めい 20-23, 夏めい 11-14)

**Key Notes**:
- めく is NEVER used standalone - always as suffix
- Tokenization varies: some noun+めく combinations are single tokens, others split
- Season verbs: 春めく and 夏めく are compound, but 冬めく splits!
- Optional noun matcher creates duplicate lower-priority matches (expected behavior)
- Pattern includes full grammatical construction based on tokenization
- All conjugations handled: めく, めいて, めいた, めいている, めいてきた, めいています

### ✓ Completed: ～てやる (do for someone / I'll do it!)

**Grammar Point**: ～てやる
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[て] + やる (やってやる, 奢ってやる, 見返してやる)
2. Verb[て] + やります (守ってやります - polite form)

**Meaning**:
- Two main usages:
  1. "Do for someone" - Casual/patronizing form of あげる (to give/do for someone)
     - Often sounds patronizing or implies the speaker is doing it because the recipient can't
     - Example: 俺がやってやる (I'll do it for you)
  2. "I'll do it!" - Expresses strong will/determination (similar to みせる)
     - Shows speaker's determination to accomplish something
     - Example: 絶対に見返してやる！(I'll definitely prove them wrong!)
- Underlying meaning emphasizes the doer being in power/control over the result

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Verb (動詞/自立/連用タ接続 or 連用形, e.g., やっ, 奢っ, 守っ, 見返し, 勝っ)
- て (助詞/接続助詞)
- やる (動詞/非自立, base=やる, 基本形 or 連用形)
- Optional: ます (助動詞) for polite form

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers for precise matching:
1. TeMatcher - Matches て as 助詞/接続助詞
2. YaruMatcher - Matches やる as 動詞/非自立 (non-self-standing verb)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- flexible_verb_form() + て + やる (動詞/非自立)
- Captures full construction including main verb in て-form

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_teyaru_benefit_standard - やってやる (9, 14) - casual benefit form
2. test_teyaru_benefit_polite - 奢ってやる (5, 10) - doing for someone
3. test_teyaru_determination - 見返してやる (8, 14) - strong will/determination
4. test_teyaru_resolve - 勝ってやる (6, 11) - determination/resolve
5. test_teyaru_polite_masu - 守ってやります (5, 12) - polite form

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Verb[て] + やる/やります:
- やってやる (9, 14) - 5 characters
- 奢ってやる (5, 10) - 5 characters
- 見返してやる (8, 14) - 6 characters
- 勝ってやる (6, 11) - 5 characters
- 守ってやります (5, 12) - 7 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes the main verb before やる (full grammatical construction)
- やる acts as auxiliary verb (動詞/非自立) in this construction
- Distinguished from standalone やる by POS tag (非自立 vs 自立)
- Handles all conjugations of やる (やる, やり+ます, etc.)
- Both meanings (benefit and determination) share same grammatical structure
- Context determines which meaning applies

### ✓ Completed: んばかりに (as if about to, seeming that it will)

**Grammar Point**: んばかりに
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[未然形] + ん + ばかり + に (言わんばかりに, 溢れんばかりの)
2. Verb[体言接続特殊] + ばかり + に/の (崩れんばかりに, 張り裂けんばかりの)

**Meaning**:
- "As if about to (A)" / "Seeming that it will (A)"
- Combination of ん (abbreviation of む, classical auxiliary verb showing intent/will) + ばかり (adverbial particle "basically exactly as described")
- Indicates that volitional form of (A) appears to be about to happen
- Similar to とばかりに but emphasizes imminent action or state
- Can be followed by に (modifying verb/action) or の (modifying noun)

**Tokenization patterns**:
**Pattern 1 - Verb[未然形] + ん auxiliary**:
- Verb (動詞/自立/未然形, e.g., 言わ, 溢れ)
- ん (助動詞/不変化型/基本形)
- ばかり (助詞/副助詞)
- に or の (助詞)

**Pattern 2 - Verb[体言接続特殊]**:
- Verb (動詞/体言接続特殊, e.g., 崩れん, 張り裂けん)
- ばかり (助詞/副助詞)
- に or の (助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers for flexible pattern matching:
1. VerbMizenOrTaigenMatcher - Matches verbs in 未然形 or 体言接続特殊 form
2. NAuxMatcher - Matches ん as auxiliary verb (optional for 体言接続特殊 case)
3. BakariMatcher - Matches ばかり as 助詞/副助詞
4. NiOrNoMatcher - Matches に or の particle (optional)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- Verb[未然形 or 体言接続特殊] + (optional ん) + ばかり + (optional に/の)
- Captures full grammatical construction including the verb

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**

3. test_nbakarini_afure - 溢れんばかりの (10, 17) - as if about to overflow (with の)
4. test_nbakarini_harisake - 張り裂けんばかりの (4, 13) - as if about to tear apart (with の)
5. test_nbakarini_tobikaka - 飛びかかんばかりに (2, 11) - as if about to jump at

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Verb + ん + ばかり + に/の:
- 崩れんばかりに (12, 19) - 7 characters (体言接続特殊 variant)
- 言わんばかりに (13, 20) - 7 characters (未然形 + ん variant)
- 溢れんばかりの (10, 17) - 7 characters (with の)
- 張り裂けんばかりの (4, 13) - 9 characters (with の)
- 飛びかかんばかりに (2, 11) - 9 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Two distinct tokenization patterns handled by same matcher
- Pattern includes the verb before ん (full grammatical construction)
- Both に and の endings supported (に for verb modification, の for noun modification)
- Classical grammar element ん (from む) expressing intent/strong will

- Handles both べからず and べからざる forms

### ✓ Completed: べからず (must not, ought not to)

**Grammar Point**: べからず
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[基本形] + べから + ず (忘れるべからず, するべからず)
2. Verb[基本形] + べから + ざる (許すべからざる, 吸うべからざる, 欠くべからざる)

**Meaning**:
- "Must not (A)" / "Ought not to (A)"
- Classical formal expression with moral/logical obligation
- Combination of べし (classical auxiliary verb "ought to") + ず/ざる (classical negative)
- べから is the 未然形 (irrealis form) of べし
- ず is the classical negative (連用ニ接続 form)
- ざる is the attributive form of ず (体言接続), used before nouns
- Modern usage: almost exclusively on signs and notices
- べからざる commonly appears in set expressions (許すべからざる, 欠くべからざる)

**Tokenization patterns**:
**べからず form**:
- Verb (動詞/基本形)
- べから (助動詞, base=べし, 未然形)
- ず (助動詞, base=ぬ, 連用ニ接続)

**べからざる form**:
- Verb (動詞/基本形)
- べから (助動詞, base=べし, 未然形)
- ざる (助動詞, base=ぬ, 体言接続)

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers for precise auxiliary verb checking:
1. BekaraMatcher - Matches べから (base=べし, 未然形)
2. ZuMatcher - Matches ず (base=ぬ, 連用ニ接続)
3. ZaruMatcher - Matches ざる (base=ぬ, 体言接続)
4. ZuOrZaruMatcher - Combines both negative forms

Pattern matcher sequence:
- Verb[基本形] + べから + (ず or ざる)
- Captures full grammatical construction

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_bekarazu_basic - 忘れるべからず (2, 9) - classic proverb
2. test_bekarazu_facility - するべからず (20, 26) - prohibition sign
3. test_bekarazaru_yurusu - 許すべからざる (21, 28) - set expression (unforgivable)
4. test_bekarazaru_suu - 吸うべからざる (0, 7) - must not smoke
5. test_bekarazaru_kaku - 欠くべからざる (8, 15) - set expression (indispensable)

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Verb + べから + ず/ざる:
- 忘れるべからず (2, 9) - 7 characters
- するべからず (20, 26) - 6 characters
- 許すべからざる (21, 28) - 7 characters
- 吸うべからざる (0, 7) - 7 characters
- 欠くべからざる (8, 15) - 7 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern does NOT include nouns that follow べからざる constructions
- The noun is a separate element being modified by the pattern
- All verbs use dictionary form (基本形) before べから
- Classical grammar still used in modern formal contexts (signs, proverbs)
- Set expressions with べからざる are common: 許す, 欠く, ありう (mostly negative moral judgments)

### ✓ Completed: に足りない (not worth / not sufficient)

**Grammar Point**: に足りない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structure**:
1. Verb[基本形] + に + 足りない (信じるにたりない, 売るにたりない, 取るにたりない)

**Meaning**:
- "Not worth (A)" / "Not sufficient for (A)"
- Formal expression used in literature or formal speech settings
- Combination of に (case particle) + 足りる (to be sufficient/enough)
- Negative form expresses inadequacy or unworthiness of something
- Common in set phrase 取るに足りない (worthless / not worth taking up for discussion)

**Tokenization pattern**:
- Verb (動詞/自立/基本形)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- たり (動詞/自立/一段/未然形, base=たりる)
- ない (助動詞/特殊・ナイ/基本形)

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers for precise POS checking:
1. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞
2. TariMatcher - Matches たり (base=たりる) as verb in 未然形
3. NaiMatcher - Matches ない as auxiliary

Pattern matcher sequence:
- Verb[基本形] + に + たり + ない
- Captures full grammatical construction

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_nitarinai_shinjiru - 信じるにたりない (19, 27) - conspiracy theorist example
2. test_nitarinai_shinrai - 信頼するにたりない (2, 11) - trust example
3. test_nitarinai_uru - 売るにたりない (6, 13) - sell example (insufficient price)
4. test_nitarinai_toru - 取るにたりない (7, 14) - idiom (worthless, not worth discussing)

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Verb + に + たり + ない:
- 信じるにたりない (19, 27) - 8 characters
- 信頼するにたりない (2, 11) - 9 characters
- 売るにたりない (6, 13) - 7 characters
- 取るにたりない (7, 14) - 7 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern works with any verb in dictionary form (基本形)
- "たりない" tokenizes as two separate tokens: たり (verb) + ない (auxiliary)
- Related pattern に足る (positive form) uses う-verb 足る, not る-verb 足りる
- Both 足りない and 足らない can appear in set phrase 取るに足りない/取るに足らない
- All test sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples

### ✓ Completed: ただ〜のみ (nothing but / all that remains)

**Grammar Point**: ただ〜のみ
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[基本形] + のみ + (だ/です/である) (待つのみだ, 祈るのみです)
2. Noun[サ変接続] + ある + のみ + (だ/です/である) (勉強あるのみだ)
3. Optional ただ prefix (ただ医者を信じて待つのみだ)

**Meaning**:
- "Nothing but (A)" / "All that remains is (A)" / "(A) is all that is required"
- Presents non-exhaustive statement that (A) is the only remaining option
- Emphasizes that one has done everything possible and all that's left is (A)
- Commonly used with verbs indicating movement, continuation, or hope
- ただ (merely/only) + のみ (adverbial particle like だけ/ばかり but more formal)

**Tokenization patterns**:
**Variant 1 - Verb + のみ**:
- Verb (動詞/基本形)
- のみ (助詞/副助詞)
- Optional copula: だ/です/である

**Variant 2 - Noun[サ変] + ある + のみ**:
- Noun (名詞/サ変接続, e.g., 勉強)
- ある (動詞/基本形, base=ある)
- のみ (助詞/副助詞)
- Optional copula: だ/です/である

**である copula**:
- で (助動詞/特殊・ダ/連用形, base=だ)
- ある (助動詞/五段・ラ行アル/基本形)

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers:
1. VerbOrNounMatcher - Matches verb[基本形] or noun
2. AruVerbMatcher - Matches ある as 動詞/基本形 (for サ変 + ある)
3. NomiMatcher - Matches のみ as 助詞/副助詞
4. DaCopulaMatcher - Matches だ (助動詞)
5. DesuCopulaMatcher - Matches です (助動詞)
6. DeCopulaMatcher - Matches で from である (助動詞/連用形)
7. AruCopulaMatcher - Matches ある from である (助動詞)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- Verb/Noun + Optional(ある) + のみ + Optional copula variants
- Captures full grammatical construction including verb/noun before のみ

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_tada_nomi_verb_with_tada - 待つのみだ (22, 27) - with ただ prefix
2. test_tada_nomi_verb_without_tada - 祈るのみです (8, 14) - polite form without ただ
3. test_tada_nomi_suru_verb_aru - 勉強あるのみだ (19, 26) - サ変 + ある construction
4. test_tada_nomi_verb_dearu - 見守るのみである (10, 18) - formal である copula
5. test_tada_nomi_verb_simple - 進むのみ (6, 10) - without copula

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full construction (Verb/Noun + ある? + のみ + copula?):
- 待つのみだ (22, 27) - 5 characters
- 祈るのみです (8, 14) - 6 characters
- 勉強あるのみだ (19, 26) - 7 characters (Noun + ある + のみだ)
- 見守るのみである (10, 18) - 8 characters
- 進むのみ (6, 10) - 4 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern includes verb/noun before のみ (full grammatical construction)
- ただ prefix is optional and contextual (not part of core pattern)
- Both regular verbs and サ変接続 + ある variants handled
- である tokenizes as で(助動詞/連用形) + ある(助動詞)
- Multiple optional matchers allow flexibility for all copula variants
- Realistic sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples

### ✓ Completed: に至っても (even when it reaches / even if it comes to)

**Grammar Point**: に至っても
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + に至（いた）っても (経つに至っても, なるに至っても)
2. Noun + に至（いた）っても (現在に至っても, 朝方に至っても, 結果に至っても)

**Meaning**:
- "Even if it came to (A), (B)" / "Even when (A), (B)"
- Presents extreme example from which some result (often negative) will not occur
- Contrast: に至っては (topic marker は) vs に至っても (even も)
- Usage: (B) often negative, showing even extreme (A) couldn't affect (B)
- に (case particle) + 至る (to climax/culminate) + て + も (adverbial particle "even")

**Tokenization patterns**:
Both verb and noun variants:
- (Verb or Noun) in dictionary form
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- 至っ (動詞/自立/五段・ラ行/連用タ接続, base=至る)
- て (助詞/接続助詞)
- も (助詞/係助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers:
1. ItatteVerbMatcher - Matches 至っ (base=至る, surface=至っ, verb)
2. TeParticleMatcher - Matches て as 助詞
3. MoParticleMatcher - Matches も as 助詞/係助詞

Pattern matcher sequence:
- Any (Verb or Noun) + に + 至っ + て + も
- Simple and clean implementation similar to に至って・に至り

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_niitattemo_verb_makikomareru - 経つに至っても (15, 22) - verb example
2. test_niitattemo_verb_naru - なるに至っても (4, 11) - verb example
3. test_niitattemo_noun_genzai - 現在に至っても (10, 17) - noun example
4. test_niitattemo_noun_asagata - 朝方に至っても (5, 12) - noun example
5. test_niitattemo_noun_kekka - 結果に至っても (17, 24) - noun example

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures (Verb/Noun) + に至っても:
- 経つに至っても (15, 22) - 7 characters
- なるに至っても (4, 11) - 7 characters
- 現在に至っても (10, 17) - 7 characters
- 朝方に至っても (5, 12) - 7 characters
- 結果に至っても (17, 24) - 7 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Both verb and noun variants use the same tokenization (に before 至る)
- Related to に至って・に至り but with も particle at the end
- も adds emphasis on "even" meaning
- Often used for regret/lament that despite reaching (A), (B) didn't change
- Can occasionally be used positively for expressing determination

### ✓ Completed: を兼ねて (also partly for the purpose of / to double as)

**Grammar Point**: を兼ねて
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + を + 兼（か）ねて (防止をかねて, 勉強をかねて, ダイエットをかねて)
2. Noun + も + 兼（か）ねて (も can replace を)

**Meaning**:
- "To (A) at the same time as (B)" / "To double as (A)" / "Also partly for the purpose of (A)"
- 兼（か）ねる = to stretch across to / to do concurrently
- Highlights (B) as the primary goal, where (A) is secondary/supporting
- Often gives speaker a good excuse to do (B)
- Literal translation: "while extending across to (A), (B)"

**Tokenization patterns**:
Standard form:
- Noun (名詞/一般 or サ変接続 or other noun types)
- を (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- かね (動詞/自立/一段/連用形, base=かねる)
- て (助詞/接続助詞)

Alternative with も:
- Noun
- も (助詞)
- かね (base=かねる)
- て (助詞/接続助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers:
1. KaneruVerbMatcher - Matches かね (base=かねる, surface=かね, verb)
2. TeParticleMatcher - Matches て as 助詞
3. WoOrMoParticleMatcher - Matches を or も as particle

Pattern matcher sequence:
- noun_matcher() + (を or も) + かね + て
- Simple and clean implementation

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_wokanete_bokeBoushi - 防止をかねて (9, 15) - prevention purpose
2. test_wokanete_benkyou - 勉強をかねて (4, 10) - study purpose
3. test_wokanete_diet - ダイエットをかねて (0, 9) - diet purpose
4. test_wokanete_multiple_nouns - 発散をかねて (9, 15) - multiple nouns with と
5. test_wokanete_taichou_kanri - 節約をかねて (5, 11) - multiple nouns with と

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Noun + を/も + かねて:
- 防止をかねて (9, 15) - 6 characters
- 勉強をかねて (4, 10) - 6 characters
- ダイエットをかねて (0, 9) - 9 characters
- 発散をかねて (9, 15) - 6 characters (from multi-noun phrase)
- 節約をかねて (5, 11) - 6 characters (from multi-noun phrase)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern works with single nouns or the last noun in a multi-noun phrase
- When multiple nouns are listed with と, pattern matches the final noun before をかねて
- Both を and も particles are supported (も emphasizes "also")
- Grammar point emphasizes (B) as primary, (A) as secondary goal
- Commonly used to justify doing something pleasurable with a practical excuse

### ✓ Completed: はさておき・はさておいて (leaving aside, apart from)

**Grammar Point**: はさておき・はさておいて
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + はさておき (感想はさておき, ことはさておき)
2. Phrase + かどうか + はさておき (あるかどうかはさておき)
3. Noun + はさておいて (冗談はさておいて) - conjunctive form with て

**Meaning**:
- "Leaving aside (A)", "Apart from (A)"
- Combination of は (adverbial particle) + さて (now, moving on) + 置き (conjunctive form of 置く - to place/leave)
- Used when (A) is topic speaker wants to move away from to discuss something more important
- Follows nouns or phrases ending with かどうか
- Set expression: 冗談はさておき (jokes aside, moving on to serious business)

**Tokenization patterns**:
はさておき form:
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- さておき (動詞/自立/五段・カ行イ音便/連用形, base=さておく)

はさておいて form:
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- さておい (動詞/自立/五段・カ行イ音便/連用タ接続, base=さておく)
- て (助詞/接続助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers:
1. HaKakariMatcher - Matches は as 助詞/係助詞
2. SateokiMatcher - Matches さておき or さておい (base=さておく, 連用形 or 連用タ接続)
3. TeMatcher - Optional て particle (助詞/接続助詞)

Pattern matcher sequence:
- は + さておき + (optional て)
- Handles both forms with single pattern declaration

- かどうかはさておき (13, 18) - 5 characters

- 冗談はさておいて (2, 8) - 6 characters (Noun + は + さておいて)

### ✓ Completed: 折には (on occasions when, when the chance comes up)

**Grammar Point**: 折には
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + おり/折 + に + (は) (いらっしゃるおりには, したおりに)
2. い-Adj + 折 + に (忙しい折に)
3. な-Adj + な + 折 + に (暇な折に)
4. Noun + の + おり/折 + に (卒業のおりに)

**Meaning**:
- "On the occasion of (A)" / "When the chance of (A) comes up"
- Formal, positive alternative for 時に
- 折 means "fold" or "pleat" - used adverbially with に to indicate an opportune time
- Primarily used for things that happen "from time to time" rather than one-off events
- Can occasionally appear with additional は (おりには)

**Tokenization patterns**:
All variants:
- おり or 折 (名詞/一般, base=おり or 折)
- に (助詞/格助詞)
- (optional) は (助詞/係助詞)

Note: After verbs, adjectives, or nouns, おり/折 is always tokenized as a noun.

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers:
1. OriMatcher - Matches おり or 折 as noun (名詞/一般)
2. NiMatcher - Matches に as 助詞/格助詞
3. HaMatcher - Optional は as 助詞/係助詞

Pattern matcher sequence:
- おり/折 + に + (optional は)
- Works after verbs, adjectives, and nouns (with の)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_oriniha_verb - いらっしゃるおりには (13, 17)
2. test_orini_verb_past - したおりに (7, 10)

4. test_orini_i_adj - 忙しい折に (4, 6)
5. test_orini_na_adj - 暇な折に (3, 5)

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures おり/折 + に + (optional は):
- いらっしゃるおりには (13, 17) - 4 characters (おりには)
- したおりに (7, 10) - 3 characters (おりに)
- 卒業のおりに (3, 6) - 3 characters (おりに)
- 忙しい折に (4, 6) - 2 characters (折に)
- 暇な折に (3, 5) - 2 characters (折に)

### ✓ Completed: ～なり～なり (either...or...)

**Grammar Point**: ～なり～なり
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Polite

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb(A) + なり + Verb(B) + なり + する (吸うなり食べるなり, 売るなり捨てるなり)
2. Noun(A) + なり + Noun(B) + なり (電話なりメールなり)
3. Verb + なり + WH-Word + Particle + Verb + なり (行くなり何をするなり)

**Undetectable structures**:
- Noun + なり + Noun + なり + に: Second なり tokenized as noun (名詞), not particle
- Noun + に + なり: The なり tokenized as verb なる (become), not particle

**Meaning**:
- "Either (A) or (B)" / "(A) or (B), whichever"
- Presents non-exhaustive list of options from which one will be chosen
- Implies other similar options in the category are also acceptable
- Different from など/や because なり emphasizes choice rather than examples
- Polite construction, formal register
- Cannot follow particles が or を

**Tokenization variants**:
- After verbs: なり is 助詞/接続助詞
- After nouns: なり is 助詞/並立助詞 or 助詞/副助詞
- Pattern must distinguish from verb なる (become)

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers to handle:
1. NariParticleMatcher - Matches なり as particle (not verb)
   - Checks surface == "なり"
   - Checks pos[0] == "助詞"
   - Checks pos[1] is one of: "接続助詞", "並立助詞", "副助詞"
2. VerbOrNounMatcher - Matches verbs or nouns before なり
3. Pattern: VerbOrNoun + なり + Wildcard(1-8) + なり

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_nari_nari_verb_verb_suru - 吸うなり弁当食べるなり (4, 15)
2. test_nari_nari_uru_suteru - 売るなり捨てるなり (6, 15)
3. test_nari_nari_noun_simple - 電話なりメールなり (5, 14)
4. test_nari_nari_verb_wh - 行くなり何をするなり (0, 10)

**Files Modified**:
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs - Implemented uff5e_nari_uff5e_nari() with 2 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs - Updated priority from 1 to 5
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs - Added uff5e_nari_uff5e_nari_tests module with 4 tests
- feature_list.json - Updated ～なり～なり to complete status

### ✓ Completed: んがため(に) (for the purpose of, in order to)

**Grammar Point**: んがため(に)
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal/Classical

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[未然形] + ん + が + ため + に (貯めんがために, 叶えんがために)
2. Verb[未然形] + ん + が + ため + の + Noun (勝たんがための, 売らんがための)
3. Exception: Causative forms use 連用形 (成功させんがために → さ+せ+ん)

**Meaning**:
- "For the purpose of (A)" / "In order to (A)" / "With (A) as the goal"
- ん comes from classical む (intent/strong will, like modern う/よう)
- Followed by が and the noun ため (sake/purpose)
- Implies speaker's will to achieve the goal
- Verb must be something controllable by the speaker

**Tokenization variants**:
**Variant 1 - Regular verbs**:
- Verb (未然形)
- ん (助動詞/不変化型/基本形)
- が (助詞/接続助詞)
- ため (名詞/非自立/副詞可能)
- に/の (助詞)

**Variant 2 - Causative forms**:
- Verb (連用形, e.g., せ from せる)
- ん (名詞/非自立/一般) **[Tokenized as noun!]**
- が (助詞/格助詞)
- ため (名詞/一般 or 非自立)
- に/の (助詞)

**Implementation**:
Created custom matchers to handle:
1. VerbBeforeNMatcher - Accepts 未然形 OR 連用形
2. NMatcher - Accepts ん as 助動詞 OR 名詞
3. GaMatcher - Accepts が as 接続助詞 OR 格助詞
4. TameMatcher - Matches ため as noun
5. NiOrNoParticleMatcher - Optional に or の

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_ngatame_ni_basic - 貯めんがために (10, 17)
2. test_ngatame_ni_kanae - 叶えんがために (13, 20)
3. test_ngatame_no_noun_katsu - 勝たんがための (15, 22)
4. test_ngatame_no_noun_uru - 売らんがための (8, 15)
5. test_ngatame_ni_suru_exception - せんがために (12, 18)

### ✓ Completed: にも～ない (can't do even if wanted to)

**Grammar Point**: にも～ない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[おう] + にも + Same Verb[potential negative] (帰ろうにも帰れない)
2. Verb[よう] + にも + Same Verb[potential negative] (寝ようにも寝れない)
3. Verb[よう] + にも + できない (連絡しようにもできない)

**Meaning**:
- "Can't (A) even if I wanted to" / "Despite wanting to (A), cannot do it"
- More specific variant of 〜に〜ない
- Volitional form (う/よう) + にも (も is required)
- Followed by potential negative form of same verb
- Tends to highlight physical/concrete reasons (vs mental limitations)

**Tokenization variants**:
**Godan verbs** (like 帰る, 買う):
- Verb (未然ウ接続, e.g., 帰ろ)
- う (助動詞)
- に (助詞/格助詞)
- も (助詞/係助詞)
- Verb potential (未然形) + ない

**Ichidan verbs** (like 寝る):
- Verb (連用形, e.g., 寝)
- よう (名詞/接尾)
- に (助詞/格助詞)
- も (助詞/係助詞)
- Verb potential (未然形) + ない

**Implementation**:
- VolitionalMatcher handles both う (godan) and よう (ichidan) forms
- Requires も particle (key distinguishing feature from general 〜に〜ない)
- Uses wildcard + NaiMatcher to capture full construction
- Pattern ends with ない to ensure complete matching

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_nimo_nai_kaeru - 帰ろうにも帰れない (33, 42)
2. test_nimo_nai_neru - 寝ようにも寝れない (19, 28)
3. test_nimo_nai_kau - 買おうにも買えない (20, 29)
4. test_nimo_nai_renraku - 連絡しようにもできない (17, 28)

**Files Modified**:
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs - Implemented nimo_uff5e_nai() with 5 custom matchers
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs - Updated priority to 5

✓ Realistic subtitle-quality sentences

### ✓ Completed: だに + しない (not even, cannot even)

**Grammar Point**: だに + しない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal/Literary

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun(サ変接続) + だ + に + し + ない (予想だにしない, 想像だにしない)
2. Noun + だに(助詞/副助詞) + Verb + ない (思いだにしない)
3. Exception: 夢にだに思わない (idiom where に + だに is part of expression)

**Meaning**:
- "Not even (A), so not (B)" / "Not (B) at all"
- Presents minimum/light examples to which (A) does not apply
- Classical particle more common in literature than modern speech
- Used in limited set of expressions: 想像だに, 考えるだに, 夢にだに, 聞くだに, 微動だに

**Tokenization (Two variants)**:
**Variant 1 - サ変接続 nouns** (予想, 想像):
- Noun (名詞/サ変接続)
- だ (助動詞/特殊・ダ/基本形)
- に (助詞/格助詞)
- し (動詞, base=する, 未然形 or 連用形)
- なかっ (助動詞, base=ない)
- た (助動詞)

**Variant 2 - Regular nouns with だに as single particle** (思い, 夢):
- Noun (名詞/一般)
- だに (助詞/副助詞) **[Single token]**
- Verb phrase (may include て, いる)
- ない (助動詞)

**Implementation**:
- Two separate matcher functions: `dani_shinai()` and `dani_shinai_particle()`
- Both registered as pattern "だに + しない" with priority 5
- First matcher: Exact sequence Noun + だ(助動詞) + に + し + ない
- Second matcher: Noun + だに(助詞/副助詞) + Wildcard(1-3) + ない
- Custom matchers for precise POS/feature checking
- Wildcard allows for verb phrases with auxiliaries (していなかった, etc.)

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_dani_shinai_yosou - 予想だにしなかった (20, 29) - サ変接続 noun
2. test_dani_shinai_souzou - 想像だにしなかった (11, 20) - サ変接続 noun
3. test_dani_shinai_omoi - 思いだにしていなかった (19, 30) - regular noun with ている
4. test_dani_shinai_yume_ni_dani - にだに思わなかった (16, 25) - idiom (includes に before だに)

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures noun + full construction:
- 予想だにしなかった (20, 29) - 9 characters
- 想像だにしなかった (11, 20) - 9 characters
- 思いだにしていなかった (19, 30) - 11 characters (includes auxiliary)
- にだに思わなかった (16, 25) - 9 characters (includes preceding に particle)

**Key Notes**:
- Kagome tokenizes だに differently depending on preceding noun type
- サ変接続 nouns: だ + に as separate tokens
- Regular nouns: だに as single 助詞/副助詞 token
- Both tokenizations represent the same grammar pattern
- 夢にだに idiom includes the に particle as part of the expression
- Pattern is classical/literary but still used in modern Japanese in fixed expressions
- Related to さえ and すら but more formal register

**Files Modified:**
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs - Implemented dani_shinai() and dani_shinai_particle()
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs - Added DaniShinai and DaniShinaiParticle with priority 5
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs - Added 4 tests in dani_shinai_tests module
- feature_list.json - Updated だに + しない to complete status

✓ Tests exist with proper assertions
✓ All tokenization variants tested (だ + に and だに forms)
✓ Realistic subtitle-quality sentences used
✓ Correct character ranges verified
✓ No print_debug() in final committed tests
✓ All existing tests still pass (3676 total, +4 new)

### ✓ Completed: に至って・に至り (only when/going as far as)

**Grammar Point**: に至って・に至り
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + に至って (されるに至って - passive verb example)
2. Noun + に至って (糖尿病に至って - noun example)
3. に至り (formal conjunctive form - ここに至り)

**Meaning**:
- "Only when (A), did (B)"
- "Going as far as to (A), (B)"
- Indicates (A) as an extreme extent from which (B) results
- Often used in criticism of lack of action (things left too long)
- Emphasizes that action only happened when situation reached extreme

**Tokenization patterns**:
Verb + に至って:
- Verb (動詞/基本形 or 一段/基本形)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- 至っ (動詞/五段・ラ行/連用タ接続, base=至る)
- て (助詞/接続助詞)

Noun + に至って:
- Noun (名詞/一般 or other noun types)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- 至っ (動詞/五段・ラ行/連用タ接続, base=至る)
- て (助詞/接続助詞)

に至り (formal):
- Noun/Pronoun (ここ, etc.)
- に (助詞/格助詞/一般)
- 至り (動詞/五段・ラ行/連用形, base=至る)

**Implementation Notes**:
- Uses TokenMatcher::Any to match either verb or noun before に
- Custom ItatteMatcher checks base_form=至る and surface=至っ/至り
- Optional te particle matcher allows both 至って (standard) and 至り (formal)
- Simple and clean implementation - pattern naturally accepts both forms

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**

4. test_niitatte_noun_age - 年に至って (2, 7) - noun (age)
5. test_niitari_formal - ここに至り (3, 8) - formal conjunctive

### ✓ Completed: たら〜で (even if, if...then with repeating words)

**Grammar Point**: たら〜で
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[たら] + Same_Verb[た] + で (なったらなったで)
2. Verb[ば] + Same_Verb[た] + で (降れば降ったで)
3. い-Adj[たら] + Same_い-Adj[基本形] + で (大きかったら大きいで)
4. い-Adj[ば] + Same_い-Adj[基本形] + で (弱ければ弱いで)
5. な-Adj + なら + Same_な-Adj + で (嫌なら嫌で)

**Meaning**:
- "Even if (A), then with (A), (B)"
- "If (A), (B)" (with contradictory result)
- Shows contradiction: good situation leads to problem, or problem can still work
- Uses repeating words to emphasize the conditional acceptance of a situation

**Implementation Notes**:
- Ideally requires matching the same word appearing twice (different conjugations)
- Due to Matcher trait constraint (single token access), matches structural pattern only
- Cannot verify base_form repetition without access to token stream
- May produce false positives but captures the most common usage patterns
- Each structure variant has its own matcher function

**Tokenization patterns**:
Verb たら form:
- Verb (動詞/連用タ接続, base=X)
- たら (助動詞/特殊・タ/仮定形, base=た)
- Verb (動詞/連用タ接続, base=X) [same base ideally]
- た (助動詞/特殊・タ/基本形, base=た)
- で (助詞/接続助詞, base=で)

Verb ば form:
- Verb (動詞/仮定形, base=X)
- ば (助詞/接続助詞, base=ば)
- Verb (動詞/連用タ接続, base=X) [same base ideally]
- た (助動詞/特殊・タ/基本形, base=た)
- で (助詞/接続助詞, base=で)

い-Adj たら form:
- い-Adj (形容詞/連用タ接続, base=X)
- たら (助動詞/特殊・タ/仮定形, base=た)
- い-Adj (形容詞/基本形, base=X) [same base ideally]
- で (助詞/接続助詞 OR 助動詞/特殊・ダ/連用形, base=で/だ)

い-Adj ば form:
- い-Adj (形容詞/仮定形, base=X)
- ば (助詞/接続助詞, base=ば)
- い-Adj (形容詞/基本形, base=X) [same base ideally]
- で (助動詞/特殊・ダ/連用形, base=だ)

な-Adj なら form:
- な-Adj (名詞/形容動詞語幹, base=X)
- なら (助動詞/特殊・ダ/仮定形, base=だ)
- な-Adj (名詞/形容動詞語幹, base=X) [same base ideally]
- で (助動詞/特殊・ダ/連用形, base=だ)

### ✓ Completed: ずにはすまない (won't get away without doing / have no choice but to do)

**Grammar Point**: ずにはすまない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb [ない-stem] + ず + には + すまない (classical form)
2. Verb [ない] + では + すまない (modern alternative)
3. Exception: する→せずにはすまない

**Meaning**:
- "Won't get away without doing (A)" / "Have no choice but to do (A)"
- Literally: "it will not come to pass that (A) is not done"
- Formal expression indicating obligation or necessity
- The action (A) is expected according to anyone, not just personal feelings
- Used when someone cannot avoid doing something due to circumstances
- Expresses strong social or moral obligation

**Tokenization**:
ずにはすまない form:
- Verb (動詞/未然形 or 未然ヌ接続 or 未然レル接続)
- ず (助動詞/特殊・ヌ/連用ニ接続, base=ぬ)
- に (助詞/格助詞)
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- すま (動詞/自立/未然形, base=すむ)
- ない (助動詞/特殊・ナイ)

ないではすまない form:
- Verb (動詞/未然形)
- ない (助動詞/特殊・ナイ)
- で (助動詞/特殊・ダ/連用形, base=だ) OR で (助詞/接続助詞, base=で) **[Two variants!]**
- は (助詞/係助詞)
- すま (動詞/自立/未然形, base=すむ)
- ない (助動詞/特殊・ナイ)

**Implementation**:
- Two separate pattern registrations: zunihasumanai() and naidewasumanai()
- Custom matcher MizenkeiVerbMatcher: Matches verbs in 未然形/未然ヌ接続/未然レル接続
- Custom matchers for ず, ない, で, and すま with strict POS/feature checks
- **Critical fix**: DeMatcher handles two tokenization variants of で:
  1. で (助動詞, base=だ) - copula form
  2. で (助詞/接続助詞, base=で) - conjunctive particle
- Both patterns have priority 5 for N1 construction
- Range includes the full verb + auxiliary + すまない construction
- Token combiner may extend range to include following だろう

**Tests Written: 6 tests (all passing)**
1. test_zunihasumanai_verb_ayamaru - 謝らずにはすまない (19, 31) - with だろう
2. test_zunihasumanai_verb_iku - 行かずにはすまない (20, 29) - regular verb
3. test_zunihasumanai_verb_deru - 出ずにはすまない (11, 19) - regular verb
4. test_zunihasumanai_suru_verb_benshou - 弁償せずにはすまない (25, 35) - する verb
5. test_zunihasumanai_naidewa_ayamaru - 謝らないではすまない (20, 30) - ないでは form
6. test_zunihasumanai_naidewa_harau - 払わないではすまない (3, 13) - ないでは form

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures verb + full construction:
- 謝らずにはすまないだろう (19, 31) - 12 characters (includes だろう via token combiner)
- 行かずにはすまない (20, 29) - 9 characters
- 出ずにはすまない (11, 19) - 8 characters
- 弁償せずにはすまない (25, 35) - 10 characters (する→せ exception)
- 謝らないではすまない (20, 30) - 10 characters (ないでは form)
- 払わないではすまない (3, 13) - 10 characters (ないでは form)

**Key Notes**:
- Reused MizenkeiVerbMatcher from ずにはおかない implementation
- Critical fix: Updated DeMatcher to handle two tokenization variants of で
- Without this fix, some sentences (like 税金は払わないではすまない) would not be detected
- Kagome inconsistently tokenizes ないで as either "ない + で(助動詞)" or "ない + で(助詞/接続助詞)"
- Two separate pattern declarations allow both forms to be detected independently
- Both forms express the same meaning, just different expression styles
- Token combiner behavior causes some tests to include trailing だろう in ranges
- Works seamlessly with various verb types (regular, する)
- Related to ずにはおかない but with すむ ("to come to pass") instead of おく ("to leave")

**Files Modified:**
- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs - Implemented zunihasumanai() and naidewasumanai()
- grammar-lib/src/patterns.rs - Added both pattern declarations with priority 5
- grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs - Added 6 tests in zunihasumanai_tests module
- feature_list.json - Updated ずにはすまない to complete status

✓ Tests exist with proper assertions
✓ All structure variants tested (ずには and ないでは forms)
✓ Realistic subtitle-quality sentences used
✓ Correct character ranges verified (with token combiner extension noted)
✓ No print_debug() in final committed tests
✓ All existing tests still pass (3661 total, +6 new)
✓ Clean git history

### ✓ Completed: ずくめ (nothing but / all in)

**Grammar Point**: ずくめ
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + ずくめ (仕事ずくめ - nothing but work)
2. Noun + ずくめ + の + Noun (黒ずくめの男 - man dressed in black)

**Meaning**:
- "Nothing but (A)" / "All in (A)" / "Completely (A)"
- Etymology: From 尽く (completely) + め suffix
- Now preferentially written as ずくめ (not づくめ)
- Expresses that (A) is to such an extent that nothing apart from (A) can be seen
- Can be used in positive or negative contexts
- Mainly used in set expressions

**Tokenization**:
All variants tokenize consistently as:
- Noun + ずくめ (名詞/接尾/一般)
- 仕事 (名詞/サ変接続) + ずくめ (名詞/接尾/一般)
- 黒 (名詞/一般) + ずくめ (名詞/接尾/一般)
- こと (名詞/非自立) + ずくめ (名詞/接尾/一般)

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: Noun + ずくめ
- Custom matcher ZukumeMatcher: Matches ずくめ as 名詞/接尾
- Uses noun_matcher() for preceding noun
- Priority 5 for N1 construction
- Range includes the full noun + ずくめ (not just ずくめ)

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_zukume_noun_shigoto - 仕事ずくめ (3, 8) - nothing but work
2. test_zukume_noun_iyanakoto - ことずくめ (5, 10) - nothing but bad things
3. test_zukume_no_noun_kuro - 黒ずくめ (2, 6) - dressed in black (with の)
4. test_zukume_no_noun_kuruma - 黒ずくめ (4, 8) - black cars (with の)

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures noun + ずくめ:
- 仕事ずくめ (3, 8) - 5 characters
- ことずくめ (5, 10) - 5 characters
- 黒ずくめ (2, 6) - 4 characters (in both の constructions)

**Key Notes**:
- Very clean implementation - just noun + suffix matcher
- Works seamlessly with の particle following (の is not part of pattern)
- Can attach to various noun types (サ変接続, 一般, 非自立, etc.)
- Pattern is suffix-like (接尾) but treated as independent construction
- Both positive and negative contexts tested

### ✓ Completed: ずじまい (end up not doing)

**Grammar Point**: ずじまい
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structure**:
1. Verb［ない］+ ず + じまい

**Meaning**:
- "End up not doing (A)" / "Not have done (A) in the end"
- Combines classical auxiliary verb ず ('not') with voiced conjunctive form of しまう (じまい)
- Expresses regret about (A) not happening
- Often followed by だった or でした (separate from pattern)
- For する verbs: する → せず (未然ヌ接続)
- For regular verbs: use 未然形 + ず

**Tokenization**:
All variants tokenize as:
- Verb (未然形 or 未然ヌ接続) + ず (助動詞) + じまい (名詞/接尾/一般)
Examples:
- 使わ (動詞, 未然形) + ず (助動詞, 特殊・ヌ) + じまい (名詞/接尾/一般)
- 行か (動詞, 未然形) + ず (助動詞, 特殊・ヌ) + じまい (名詞/接尾/一般)
- せ (動詞, 未然ヌ接続) + ず (助動詞, 特殊・ヌ) + じまい (名詞/接尾/一般) [for する verbs]

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: Verb (未然形/未然ヌ接続) + ず + じまい
- Three custom matchers:
  1. MizenVerbMatcher: Matches verbs in 未然形 or 未然ヌ接続
  2. ZuAuxiliaryMatcher: Matches ず (助動詞) with base form ぬ
  3. JimaiMatcher: Matches じまい as 名詞/接尾
- Priority 5 for N1 construction
- Range includes verb + ず + じまい (token combiner may extend to include だった)

- 使わずじまいだった (36, 45) - 9 characters (includes だった via token combination)
- 告白せずじまい (5, 12) - 7 characters (する verb becomes せず)

**Key Notes**:
- Classical Japanese construction using literary auxiliary ず
- する verbs use 未然ヌ接続 form (せず, not しず)
- Regular verbs use standard 未然形 (使わず, 行かず, etc.)
- Token combiner automatically extends range when followed by だった/でした
- This is expected behavior - similar to how そうだ includes both parts
- Pattern emphasizes regret about something not happening
- Very formal/literary register despite modern usage

### ✓ Completed: そばから (as soon as / right after)

**Grammar Point**: そばから
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[る] + そばから (dictionary form: するそばから)
2. Verb[た] + そばから (past form: 直したそばから)

**Meaning**:
- "As soon as (A), (B)" / "Right after (A), (B)"
- Etymology: そば (side) + から (from) = "from right beside (A)"
- Usually (B) is something annoying or unfavorable that nullifies (A)
- Implies that (B) always happens upon (A), not a one-off situation
- Cannot be used for once-off situations

**Tokenization**:
All variants tokenize consistently as:
- **Dictionary form**: Verb (基本形) + そば + から
  - する (動詞, 基本形) + そば (名詞) + から (助詞/格助詞)
- **Past form**: Verb (連用形/連用タ接続) + た/だ + そば + から
  - 直し (動詞, 連用形) + た (助動詞) + そば (名詞) + から (助詞/格助詞)

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: Verb + (optional た/だ) + そば + から
- Custom matchers:
  1. VerbBeforeSobaMatcher: Matches verbs in 基本形, 連用形, or 連用タ接続
  2. SobaMatcher: Matches そば (名詞/一般)
  3. KaraParticleMatcher: Matches から (助詞/格助詞)
- Uses TokenMatcher::Optional for past auxiliary た/だ
- Priority 5 for N1 formal construction
- Range includes the verb + そばから

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**

3. test_sobakara_verb_past_kowareru - 直したそばから (5, 12) - breaks as soon as fixed
4. test_sobakara_verb_past_wasureru - 読んだそばから (8, 15) - forgets right after reading

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures verb + そばから:
- するそばから (6, 12) - 6 characters (する + そばから)
- 注意するそばから (2, 10) - 8 characters (注意する + そばから)
- 直したそばから (5, 12) - 7 characters (直した + そばから)
- 読んだそばから (8, 15) - 7 characters (読んだ + そばから)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern captures full verb phrase (including サ変 nouns for する verbs)
- Works with both dictionary form (基本形) and past form (た/だ)
- The pattern emphasizes immediate succession and recurring events
- Very clean tokenization - just verb + そば + から
- Formal register - common in explanations and narratives
- Often describes frustrating situations where effort is immediately undone

### ✓ Completed: でもなんでもない (not at all / definitely not)

**Grammar Point**: でもなんでもない
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + でもなんでもない (ファンでもなんでもない)
2. な-Adjective + でもなんでもない (迷惑でもなんでもない)
3. い-Adjective + くもなんでもない (重くもなんでもない - less common)
4. Polite forms: + ないです
5. Casual forms: + ないじゃん

**Meaning**:
- "Definitely not (A)" / "Not (A) at all"
- Combines でも "even" + なんでも "anything" + ない "not"
- Literal: "not even (A) or anything of the sort"
- Can be used positively (no big deal) or negatively (strong denial)

**Tokenization (Three variants)**:
1. **Noun pattern**: Noun + で + も + なん + で + も + ない
   - で and も are separate tokens (助詞/格助詞 and 助詞/係助詞)
   
2. **な-Adjective pattern**: な-Adj + でも + なん + で + も + ない
   - でも tokenizes as single token (助詞/副助詞)
   
3. **い-Adjective pattern**: い-Adj[く] + も + なん + で + も + ない
   - No で before the first も (くも, not くでも)

**Implementation**:
- Three separate matcher functions for different tokenizations:
  1. `demonandemonai()`: [Any] + で + も + なん + で + も + ない (nouns)
  2. `demonandemonai_demo()`: [Any] + でも + なん + で + も + ない (な-adj)
  3. `demonandemonai_kumo()`: [Any] + も + なん + で + も + ない (い-adj く-form)
- All three patterns registered in patterns.rs with same name "でもなんでもない"
- Priority 5 for all variants (N1 construction)
- Custom matchers for precise POS matching:
  - DeParticleMatcher: で (助詞/格助詞)
  - MoParticleMatcher: も (助詞/係助詞)
  - NanNounMatcher: なん (名詞/代名詞)
  - NaiAdjectiveMatcher: ない (形容詞/自立)
  - DemoParticleMatcher: でも (助詞/副助詞)

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_demonandemonai_noun_fan - ファンでもなんでもない (2, 13) - noun form
2. test_demonandemonai_noun_friend - 友達でもなんでもないです (10, 22) - noun + です
3. test_demonandemonai_na_adj_bothersome - 迷惑でもなんでもないです (0, 12) - な-adj + です
4. test_demonandemonai_na_adj_like - 好きでもなんでもない (0, 10) - な-adj plain
5. test_demonandemonai_i_adj_heavy - 重くもなんでもないじゃん (2, 14) - い-adj + じゃん

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full construction:
- ファンでもなんでもない (2, 13) - 11 characters
- 友達でもなんでもないです (10, 22) - 12 characters (includes polite です)
- 迷惑でもなんでもないです (0, 12) - 12 characters (includes polite です)
- 好きでもなんでもない (0, 10) - 10 characters
- 重くもなんでもないじゃん (2, 14) - 12 characters (includes casual じゃん)

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern automatically includes です/じゃん when present (grammatically connected)
- Three tokenization variants require three separate matcher functions
- でも can be one token (副助詞) or two tokens (で + も) depending on context
- い-Adjective variant uses くも (not くでも)
- Less common い-Adj variant included for completeness
- Standard register - used in both formal and informal contexts

### ✓ Completed: とあって (since / because of)

**Grammar Point**: とあって
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + とあって (行われるとあって - "since it will be held")
2. い-Adjective + とあって (涼しいとあって - "since it's cool")
3. な-Adjective + だ + とあって (便利だとあって - "since it's convenient")
4. Noun + とあって (最終日とあって - "since it's the final day")

**Meaning**:
- "Since (A), it is no wonder that (B)" / "Because of (A), it's natural that (B)"
- Presents an unusual situation from which an obvious result occurs
- Formal register - used in written and formal speech
- Only used objectively - does not refer to the speaker themselves

**Tokenization**:
All variants tokenize consistently as:
- [Preceding word]: Verb (基本形), い-Adj (基本形), な-Adj stem, or Noun
- Optional: だ (助動詞, 基本形) for な-Adj and some nouns
- と: 助詞/格助詞/引用 (quotative) or 助詞/格助詞/一般 (general case)
- あっ: 動詞/自立, base=ある, 連用タ接続 (te-form of ある)
- て: 助詞/接続助詞 (conjunctive particle)

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: [Any] + と + あっ + て
- Custom matchers:
  1. ToParticleMatcher: Matches と (助詞/格助詞)
  2. AtteMatcher: Matches あっ (verb ある in 連用タ接続)
  3. TeParticleMatcher: Matches て (助詞/接続助詞)
- TokenMatcher::Any captures the preceding word (verb/adj/noun)
- Priority 5 for N1 formal construction
- Range includes the full grammatical unit (preceding word + とあって)

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**

4. test_toatte_noun - 日とあって (5, 10) - noun form

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures preceding word + とあって:
- れるとあって (15, 21) - 6 characters (verb + とあって)
- 涼しいとあって (9, 16) - 7 characters (i-adj + とあって)
- だとあって (6, 11) - 5 characters (copula + とあって)
- 日とあって (5, 10) - 5 characters (noun + とあって)

**Key Notes**:
- Very straightforward pattern - just と particle + te-form of ある
- Works with verbs, adjectives, and nouns (with optional だ)
- The pattern is similar to として "as" but focuses on what (A) is viewed as
- Must be used objectively - cannot refer to the speaker
- Common in formal explanations and news reports

### Session Summary - こととて Complete ✓
**Status**: Implemented こととて pattern successfully

**Session highlights**:
- Implemented こととて (due to / because of)

- Used Wildcard matcher with stop conditions to capture appropriate context

### ✓ Completed: こととて (due to / because of)

**Grammar Point**: こととて
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + こととて (起こったこととて - "due to it having happened")
2. Verb[ない] + ぬ + こととて (知らぬこととて - "due to not knowing", classical negative)
3. Noun + の + こととて (新人のこととて - "due to being a newbie")

**Meaning**:
- "Due to (A)" / "Because of (A)"
- Classical/formal expression for presenting reasons
- Almost exclusively used in writing in modern Japanese
- Primarily used when asking for forgiveness
- (A) is typically something for which negative result of (B) is obvious/expected

**Etymology**:
- こと = noun "thing"
- とて = binding particle for presenting reasons
- とて thought to be combination of と (case particle) + て (conjunction particle)
- Or possibly conjunctive form of classical auxiliary verb たり

**Tokenization**:
All variants tokenize consistently as:
- こと: 名詞/非自立/一般 (dependent noun)
- と: 助詞/格助詞/引用 (quotative case particle)
- て: 助詞/接続助詞 (conjunctive particle)

Preceding context varies:
- Verb patterns: Verb + た (past auxiliary) or ぬ (classical negative)
- Noun pattern: Noun/Adverb + の (nominalizer particle)

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: [Wildcard 1-2 tokens] + こと + と + て
- Wildcard captures 1-2 tokens before こととて to include the reason clause
- Stop conditions prevent capturing beyond sentence boundaries:
  - Stops at case particles (に, を, で, etc.) except の
  - Stops at topic/binding particles (は, も)
  - Does NOT stop at adverbs/nouns (allows patterns like "突然のこととて")
- Custom matchers:
  1. KotoMatcher: Matches こと (名詞/非自立)
  2. ToParticleMatcher: Matches と (助詞/格助詞)
  3. TeParticleMatcher: Matches て (助詞/接続助詞)
  4. StopAtSentenceBoundary: Stop condition for wildcard expansion
- Priority 5 for N1 formal construction
- Range includes the reason clause + こととて

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. test_kototote_verb_past - 起こったこととて (10, 18) - due to having happened
2. test_kototote_verb_nu_negative - 知らぬこととて (9, 16) - due to not knowing
3. test_kototote_noun_no_newbie - 新人のこととて (0, 7) - due to being a newbie
4. test_kototote_noun_no_sudden - 突然のこととて (0, 7) - due to suddenness

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures reason clause + こととて:
- 起こったこととて (10, 18) - 8 characters (Verb + た + こととて)
- 知らぬこととて (9, 16) - 7 characters (Verb + ぬ + こととて)
- 新人のこととて (0, 7) - 7 characters (Noun + の + こととて)
- 突然のこととて (0, 7) - 7 characters (Adverb + の + こととて)

**Key Implementation Notes**:
- Initially tried TokenMatcher::Any but only captured 1 token
- Switched to Wildcard with min:1, max:3 but captured too much context
- Final solution: Wildcard with min:1, max:2 plus stop conditions
- Stop conditions crucial to prevent including unrelated particles/adverbs
- Max of 2 tokens prevents patterns like "について あまり 知らぬ" (3 tokens)
- Allows "突然 の" (2 tokens) and "知ら ぬ" (2 tokens) correctly
- Example stop behavior: "商品について|あまり知らぬこととて" stops at について

- feature_list.json - Updated こととて to complete status

✓ Realistic subtitle-quality sentences used

- All structure variants tested with realistic subtitle sentences
- Pattern ranges include full construction (Noun + ぐるみで/の)

### ✓ Completed: ぐるみで (including / all over / whole)

**Grammar Point**: ぐるみで
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + ぐるみで (家族ぐるみで - "with the entire family")
2. Noun + ぐるみの + Noun (家族ぐるみの付き合い - "family-wide friendship")

**Meaning**:
- "Including (A)", "all over (A)", or "(A) wide"
- Expresses that something encompasses absolutely all of (A)
- Etymology: From 包（くる）む "to wrap/encompass"
- Often used with group/collective nouns (family, company, town, etc.)
- Emphasizes that the group shares a common trait, not individual parts

**Tokenization**:
- Noun: 名詞/一般
- ぐるみ: 名詞/接尾/一般 (noun suffix)
- で: 助詞/格助詞/一般 (case particle "with/by")
- の: 助詞/連体化 (nominalizer - when connecting to another noun)

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: [Any] + ぐるみ + (で/の)
- Custom matchers:
  1. GurumiMatcher: Matches ぐるみ (名詞/接尾/一般)
  2. DeNoMatcher: Matches で (助詞/格助詞) OR の (助詞/連体化)
- Priority 5 for N1 standard construction
- Pattern includes the preceding noun
- Range: Noun + ぐるみで/の (e.g., 家族ぐるみで, 街ぐるみの)

1. test_gurumide_family_with_de - 家族ぐるみで (2, 8) - with entire family
2. test_gurumide_company_with_de - 会社ぐるみで (0, 6) - entire company
3. test_gurumide_family_with_no - 家族ぐるみの (3, 9) - family-wide (+ noun)
4. test_gurumide_town_with_no - 街ぐるみの (0, 5) - town-wide (+ noun)

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Noun + ぐるみで/の:
- 家族ぐるみで (2, 8) - 6 characters
- 会社ぐるみで (0, 6) - 6 characters
- 家族ぐるみの (3, 9) - 6 characters
- 街ぐるみの (0, 5) - 5 characters

**Key Notes**:
- ぐるみ tokenizes as noun suffix (名詞/接尾), not as a standalone verb
- で marks the method/means ("with/by means of the whole group")
- の connects to following nouns to form noun phrases
- Both particles handled by single DeNoMatcher for simplicity
- Commonly used with collective nouns: 家族 (family), 会社 (company), 街 (town), etc.
- Standard register - used in both formal and informal contexts

### ✓ Completed: ってば・ったら (insisting viewpoint / addressing person)

**Grammar Point**: ってば・ったら
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Casual

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + ってば (分かったってば - "I said I understand!")
2. い-Adjective + ってば (痛いってば - "I said it hurts!")
3. な-Adjective/Noun + だ + ってば (嫌いだってば - "I said I hate it!")
4. Noun + だ + ったら (大人だったら - "I said I'm an adult!")
5. Person name + ってば (金太郎ってば - "That darn Kintaro!")

**Meaning**:
- "I said (A)!" / "I told you (A)!" - Insisting on one's viewpoint
- "That darn (A)" / "Oh that (A)" - Expressing frustration about someone
- Contraction of といえば/といったら "if it were said"
- Very casual register, common in conversation

**Tokenization (Three variants)**:
1. って + ば: [Any] + って (助詞/格助詞/連語) + ば (助詞/接続助詞)
2. だって + ば: [Any] + だって (助詞/副助詞) + ば (助詞/接続助詞)
3. だっ + たら: [Any] + だっ (助動詞 だ, 連用タ接続) + たら (助動詞 た, 仮定形)

**Implementation**:
- Three separate matcher functions to handle tokenization variants:
  1. tteba_u30fb_ttara(): [Any] + って + ば
  2. tteba_u30fb_ttara_datte(): [Any] + だって + ば
  3. tteba_u30fb_ttara_dattara(): [Any] + だっ + たら
- All three patterns registered with same name "ってば・ったら"
- Priority 1 for all variants (N1 construction)
- Uses 6 custom matchers for precise POS tag matching

**Tests Written: 5 tests (all passing)**
1. test_tteba_verb_past - たってば (3, 7) - "I said I understand!"
2. test_tteba_i_adjective - 痛いってば (0, 5) - "I said it hurts!"
3. test_tteba_noun_with_da - 嫌いだってば (0, 6) - "I said I hate it!"
4. test_ttara_noun_with_da - 大人だったら (6, 12) - "I said I'm an adult!"
5. test_tteba_person_name_addressing - 金太郎ってば (0, 6) - "That darn Kintaro!"

**Undetectable cases (documented with TODO comments)**:
1. **い-Adj + ったら** (e.g., 痛いったら):
   - Tokenizes as: 痛 (adj stem) + いっ (verb いう) + たら (conditional)
   - Cannot distinguish from actual verb いう + たら
   - Kagome treats いったら as verb "to say" in conditional form

2. **あなたったら** (pronoun "you" + ったら):
   - Tokenizes as: あな (noun "hole") + たっ (verb たつ "to stand") + たら
   - Should be: あなた (pronoun "you") + ったら
   - Kagome tokenization error

### ✓ Completed: ずとも (even if not / don't have to)

**Grammar Point**: ずとも
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[ない] + ず + とも (standard verb form)
2. Noun + ならずとも (from なる[ない] + ず + とも)
3. Exception: する → せずとも
4. Optional: + 良い (せずとも良い = "okay even without")

**Meaning**:
- "Even if not (A), (B)" / "Don't have to (A) to (B)"
- Classical negative ず + conjunction particle とも
- Formal/literary expression
- Can be used sarcastically

**Tokenization (Two variants)**:
1. **Merged とも** (single token):
   - Verb: 動詞/自立, 未然形 or 未然ヌ接続
   - ず: 助動詞, base=ぬ, 特殊・ヌ, 連用ニ接続
   - とも: 助詞/接続助詞

2. **Split と + も** (two tokens):
   - Verb: 動詞/自立, 未然形 or 未然ヌ接続
   - ず: 助動詞, base=ぬ
   - と: 助詞/格助詞
   - も: 助詞/係助詞

**Implementation**:
- Two patterns to handle tokenization variants:
  1. `zutomo()`: Verb + ず + とも (single token)
  2. `zutomo_split()`: Verb + ず + と + も (split tokens)
- Custom matchers:
  - NaiStemVerbMatcher: Matches verbs in 未然形 or 未然ヌ接続
  - ZuMatcher: Matches ず (助動詞, base=ぬ)
  - TomoMatcher: Matches とも as single token
  - ToParticleMatcher + MoParticleMatcher: For split tokenization
- Priority 1 for both patterns (N1 construction)
- Both patterns registered in patterns.rs with same name "ずとも"

**Tests Written: 6 tests (all passing)**
1. `test_zutomo_verb_regular` - 練習せずとも (2, 8) - without practicing (する verb)
2. `test_zutomo_verb_kiku` - 聞かずとも (23, 28) - without hearing (u-verb)
3. `test_zutomo_verb_study` - 留学せずとも (3, 9) - without studying abroad (する verb)
4. `test_zutomo_narazu_noun` - ならずとも (3, 8) - without being (noun + なる)
5. `test_zutomo_narazu_parent` - ならずとも (1, 6) - without being parent (noun + なる)
6. `test_zutomo_with_yoi` - せずとも良い (24, 30) - okay without practicing

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full verb phrase:
- 練習せずとも (2, 8) - includes サ変 noun for する verbs
- 聞かずとも (23, 28) - just the verb + ず + とも
- 留学せずとも (3, 9) - includes サ変 noun
- ならずとも (3, 8) - なる verb negated
- ならずとも (1, 6) - shorter position

**Key Notes**:
- Classical negative auxiliary ず (not modern ない)
- とも can tokenize two ways depending on context
- For する verbs, range includes the noun (練習せずとも)
- ならずとも used after nouns meaning "without being X"
- More formal/literary than modern ないでも
- Often followed by 良い (良かろう in classical form)

### ✓ Completed: にかかっている (depends on)

**Grammar Point**: にかかっている
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + に + かかっている
2. Phrase + かどうか + に + かかっている
3. WH-Word + Phrase + か + に + かかっている
4. Polite forms: Same structures with にかかっています

**Meaning**:
- "To depend on (A)" / "To rest on (A)"
- Shows that something is contingent upon or determined by something else
- Literally: かかる = "to hang on", so "hanging on" = "depending on"
- May optionally include 如何（いかん）before に for emphasis

**Tokenization**:
- に: 助詞/格助詞/一般 (case particle)
- かかっ: 動詞/自立, base=かかる, 五段・ラ行, 連用タ接続
- て: 助詞/接続助詞
- いる: 動詞/非自立, base=いる, 一段 (auxiliary verb)
- ます (polite): 助動詞, base=ます, 特殊・マス

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: に + かかっ + て + いる
- Three custom matchers:
  1. NiParticleMatcher: Matches に (助詞/格助詞)
  2. KakaruVerbMatcher: Matches かかる in 連用タ接続 form
  3. TeParticleMatcher: Matches て (助詞/接続助詞)
- Plus specific_verb("いる") for the auxiliary
- Priority 5 for N1 construction
- Handles both いる and います naturally (ます attaches to いる)

- にかかっている (8, 15) - 7 characters
- にかかっている (25, 32) - 7 characters
- にかかっている (23, 30) - 7 characters
- にかかっています (26, 34) - 8 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Pattern starts from the に particle (case marker)
- The に indicates what something depends on
- かかる is the verb "to hang/depend"
- Standard continuous form (かかっている) most common
- Polite form (かかっています) handled automatically
- Often preceded by かどうか or question word + か
- Formal/written register - common in formal explanations

**Session highlights**:

- Handles TWO different tokenization patterns:
  1. Noun + なくし(verb) + て + は
  2. こと + なく(adjective) + し(verb) + て + は

### ✓ Completed: なくして(は) (without - if not for A, then not B)

**Grammar Point**: なくして(は)
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Noun + なくして(は)
2. Verb + ことなくして(は)

**Meaning**:
- "If not for (A), then not (B)" / "Without (A), (B) is impossible"
- Hypothetical expression showing (B) cannot be achieved without (A)
- May also appear as なしには or なしでは (less formal variants)
- The determination comes across strongly - emphasizes impossibility
- Etymology: なく (ない adjective conjunctive form) + して (conjunction particle) + は

**Tokenization**:

**Pattern 1 (Noun + なくして(は))**:
- Noun: 名詞 (any noun)
- なくし: 動詞/自立, base=なくす, 連用形 (verb "to lose/lack")
- て: 助詞/接続助詞
- は: 助詞/係助詞 (optional)

**Pattern 2 (Verb + ことなくして(は))**:
- こと: 名詞/非自立/一般 (nominalizer)
- なく: 形容詞/自立, base=ない, 連用テ接続 (adjective "not" in conjunctive form)
- し: 動詞/自立, base=する, 連用形 (verb "to do")
- て: 助詞/接続助詞
- は: 助詞/係助詞 (optional)

**Implementation**:
- Single matcher handles both tokenization patterns elegantly
- Pattern: [Any] + なくし/なく + (optional し) + て + (optional は)
- Custom matchers:
  - NakushiNakuMatcher: Matches both なくし (verb なくす) OR なく (adjective ない)
  - ShiVerbMatcher: Matches し (verb する) - made optional to handle both patterns
  - TeMatcher: Matches て (助詞/接続助詞)
  - HaMatcher: Matches は (助詞/係助詞) - made optional as は can be omitted
- Priority 5 for N1 formal construction
- Pattern includes the full grammatical unit
- Range: Noun/こと + なくして(は)

**Tests Written: 4 tests (all passing)**
1. `test_nakushiteha_noun_simple` - 彼女なくしては (2, 9) - without her
2. `test_nakushiteha_noun_effort` - 努力なくしては (0, 7) - without effort
3. `test_nakushiteha_verb_koto` - ことなくしては (4, 11) - without failing
4. `test_nakushiteha_verb_koto_consider` - ことなくしては (10, 17) - without considering

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures full construction:
- 彼女なくしては (2, 9) - 7 characters (includes noun)
- 努力なくしては (0, 7) - 7 characters (includes noun)
- ことなくしては (4, 11) - 7 characters (includes こと)
- ことなくしては (10, 17) - 7 characters (includes こと)

**Key Notes**:
- Two different tokenization patterns handled by single matcher using Optional
- なくす verb pattern: Direct attachment to nouns (彼女なくして)
- ない adjective pattern: Used with nominalized verbs (考えることなく)
- Both patterns mean "without" but have different grammatical structures
- The optional し token is the key to handling both patterns
- Pattern 1: なくし + て (no し needed - it's part of なくし)
- Pattern 2: なく + し + て (し is separate token)
- The (は) is optional in both patterns
- Formal register - rarely used in casual speech
- Always followed by negative consequence in second clause

### ✓ Completed: まま(に)1 (as one wishes / on a whim)

**Grammar Point**: まま(に)1
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[dictionary] + (が) + まま(に)

**Meaning**:
- "To do (A) as one wishes/desires" / "To do (A) on a whim"
- Indicates letting the action of (A) happen in whatever way is most natural
- Not making any particular effort to control the outcome
- Different from basic まま(に) which means "left in the state of"

**Common expressions**:
- 思うままに = as one thinks/wishes
- 思いつくままに = whatever comes to mind
- 気の向くままに = as one's interest is inclined
- 足の向くままに = wherever one's feet take them
- 欲するままに = as one desires
- 風の吹くままに = in whatever way the wind blows

**Tokenization**:
- Verb: 動詞 (基本形 - dictionary form)
- が: 助詞/格助詞 (optional)
- まま: 名詞/非自立/副詞可能
- に: 助詞/格助詞/一般 (optional)

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: Verb[基本形] + (が) + まま + (に)
- Custom matchers:
  - DictionaryFormVerbMatcher: Matches 動詞 with features[5] = 基本形
  - GaParticleMatcher: Matches が (助詞/格助詞) - optional

### ✓ Completed: ぐらいなら (would rather / better off)

**Grammar Point**: ぐらいなら
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[dictionary] + ぐらいなら + Phrase
2. Verb[dictionary] + くらいなら + Phrase (alternative)

**Meaning**:
- "Would rather (B) than (A)" / "Better off (B) than (A)"
- Literal: "If it gets to the extent of (A), then (B)" where (B) is preferable
- Combines くらい 'about/extent' and なら 'if' (conditional)
- Often used with suggestions/opinions like ほうがいい, ほうがましだ

**Tokenization**:
- Verb: 動詞 (基本形 - dictionary form)
- ぐらい/くらい: 助詞/副助詞 (adverbial particle)
- なら: 助動詞, base=だ, 仮定形 (conditional auxiliary)

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: Verb[基本形] + ぐらい/くらい + なら
- Custom matchers:
  - GuraiKuraiMatcher: Matches ぐらい or くらい (助詞/副助詞)
  - NaraMatcher: Matches なら (助動詞, base=だ, 仮定形)
- Priority 5 for N1 standard construction
- Pattern includes the full grammatical unit
- Range: Verb + ぐらいなら/くらいなら

### ✓ Completed: がてら (while doing, on the occasion of)

**Grammar Point**: がてら
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb[stem] + がてら
2. Noun + がてら

**Meaning**:
- "While (A), (B)" / "On the occasion of (A), (B)"
- Unlike ながら, focuses on (A) being a good opportunity to do (B)
- Etymology: がてら comes from かてあり (糅つ "to mix" + あり)
- Used primarily in formal situations
- (A) is typically a noun signifying physical movement

**Tokenization**:
- [Verb/Noun]: 動詞 (連用形) or 名詞
- が: 助詞/格助詞/一般 (case particle)
- てら: 動詞/自立, base=てる, 未然形

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: [Any] + が + てら
- Custom matchers:
  - GaParticleMatcher: Matches が (助詞/格助詞)
  - TeraMatcher: Matches てら (動詞, base=てる, 未然形)
- Priority 5 for N1 formal construction
- Pattern includes the verb/noun before がてら
- Range: Verb/Noun + がてら (e.g., 迎えがてら, 散歩がてら)

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Verb/Noun + がてら:
- 迎えがてら (5, 10) - 5 characters
- 行きがてら (5, 10) - 5 characters
- 散歩がてら (6, 11) - 5 characters
- 運動がてら (0, 5) - 5 characters

**Key Notes**:
- Kagome consistently tokenizes がてら as が + てら (never as single token)
- Similar to ながら but emphasizes utilizing an opportunity
- The preceding noun is typically a physical movement (散歩, 運動, etc.)
- Formal register - common in written/formal speech
- Pattern successfully distinguishes from other が particle uses

- All 3 structure variants tested with realistic subtitle sentences

### ✓ Completed: べくして (as expected, destined to)

**Grammar Point**: べくして
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5
**Register**: Formal

**Pattern structures**:
1. (Intransitive) Verb + べくして + (same Verb)
2. Verb (passive られる) + べくして + (same Verb)

**Meaning**:
- "As expected of (A), (B)" / "As was supposed to (A), (B)"
- Indicates something happened as a natural/logical course of events
- The same verb appears twice: before and after べくして
- Example: 勝つべくして勝った = "Won as (we were) expected to win"
- べく (classical auxiliary) shows purpose/end goal
- して adds conjunctive "and then" meaning
- Quite formal - rarely used in daily speech

**Tokenization**:
- Verb: 動詞/自立 (basic form 基本形)
- べく: 助動詞, base=べし, 文語・ベシ, 連用形
- し: 動詞/自立, base=する, サ変・スル, 連用形
- て: 助詞/接続助詞

**Implementation**:
- Pattern: [Any] + べく + し + て
- Custom matchers:
  - BekuMatcher: Matches べく (助動詞, base=べし)
  - ShiMatcher: Matches し (動詞 from する)
  - TeMatcher: Matches て (助詞/接続助詞)
- Priority 5 for N1 formal construction
- Pattern includes the verb before べくして
- Range: Verb + べくして (e.g., 勝つべくして)

**Tests Written: 3 tests (all passing)**
1. `test_bekushite_intransitive_verb` - 勝つべくして (18, 24) - expected to win
2. `test_bekushite_intransitive_verb_happen` - 起こるべくして (25, 32) - expected to happen
3. `test_bekushite_intransitive_verb_meet` - 出会うべくして (7, 14) - destined to meet

All tests use realistic subtitle-quality sentences from grammar_points_data.json examples.

**Character Ranges**: Pattern captures Verb + べくして:
- 勝つべくして (18, 24) - 6 characters
- 起こるべくして (25, 32) - 7 characters (起こる is 3 chars)
- 出会うべくして (7, 14) - 7 characters (出会う is 3 chars)

**Key Notes**:
- Classical auxiliary verb べし derives from 可 (permissible/allowed)
- Indicates situation that doesn't exist yet but is certain to come about
- べくして construction emphasizes inevitability and proper order of events
- Pattern includes the preceding verb in its range
- Same verb appears after べくして (not included in pattern range)
- Note: Current implementation matches べくして but doesn't validate verb repetition
  (similar limitation to という1 - would require cross-token comparison)

### ✓ Completed: ことだし (since/because, listing one reason among others)

**Grammar Point**: ことだし
**JLPT Level**: N1
**Priority**: 5 (simple form: 1, compound form: 2)
**Register**: Standard

**Pattern structures**:
1. Verb + ことだし
2. い-Adjective + ことだし
3. な-Adjective + な + ことだし
4. Noun + の + ことだし

**Meaning**:
- "Since (A)" / "Because (A)"
- Emphasizes that there are OTHER reasons besides what's stated
- Used for listing reasons or justifications
- Pattern: [Reason A] + ことだし, [Action/Conclusion]
- Implies: "While there are other reasons, (A) is good enough reason to (B)"

**Tokenization**:
Simple form (Verb/い-Adj):
- Verb/Adjective: 動詞 or 形容詞 (base form)
- こと: 名詞/非自立/一般 (noun, non-autonomous)
- だ: 助動詞, base=だ (auxiliary verb)
- し: 助詞/接続助詞 (conjunctive particle)

Compound form (Noun/な-Adj):
- Noun or な-Adj stem: 名詞
- の or な: 助詞/連体化 or 助動詞 (連体接続)
- こと: 名詞/非自立/一般
- だ: 助動詞
- し: 助詞/接続助詞

**Implementation**:
Two separate patterns to handle different tokenization structures:

1. **ことだし (simple)**: Priority 1
   - Pattern: [Any token] + こと + だ + し
   - Matches: Verb + ことだし, い-Adjective + ことだし
   - Example ranges: 来ることだし (16-22), いいことだし (3-9)

2. **ことだし_compound**: Priority 2 (higher - more specific)
   - Pattern: [Any token] + の/な + こと + だ + し
   - Matches: Noun + の + ことだし, な-Adj + な + ことだし
   - Example ranges: 彼のことだし (0-6), 有名なことだし (10-17)
   - Custom matcher for の (助詞/連体化) or な (助動詞/体言接続)

Technical Implementation:
- Matcher functions: kotodashi() and kotodashi_compound() in grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs
- Custom matchers for precise POS tag matching (KotoMatcher, DaMatcher, ShiMatcher, NoNaMatcher)
- Pattern type: Construction pattern
- Two-pattern approach handles different syntactic structures cleanly

Test Details:
- Module: kotodashi_tests in grammar-lib/src/tests/n1_patterns.rs
- Test count: 4 comprehensive tests
- Structure variants tested:
  * Verb + ことだし (来ることだし)
  * い-Adjective + ことだし (いいことだし)
  * な-Adjective + な + ことだし (有名なことだし)
  * Noun + の + ことだし (彼のことだし)
- All tests include proper assertions and character range verification
- No print_debug() in final committed code

- grammar-lib/src/matchers/n1.rs - Added kotodashi() and kotodashi_compound()
